### [1] [VAR-Q: Tuning-free KV Cache Quantization for Visual Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05380)
*Boxun Xu,Jiaji Lu,Yuxuan Yin,Zihu Wang,Ziyue Liu,Yu Wang,Zirui Liu,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 分析视觉自回归生成中KV缓存的离群值结构，提出免调优的KV缓存量化框架VAR-Q，在减少78.5% KV内存的同时保持质量基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自回归生成（图像/视频的next-scale预测与视频的next-frame预测）按块而非逐token解码，视觉token量巨大，导致KV缓存规模在扩展时超过模型权重，成为大规模部署的瓶颈。而现有面向语言模型设计的KV缓存压缩方法未考虑其与LLM缓存统计特性的差异。

Method: 先对视觉自回归KV缓存进行实证分析：发现少数大值离群通道承载主要功能（消融其导致生成崩溃，而消融同等规模的非离群通道无影响）；离群值沿特征与块两个轴向组织，单元格内幅值稳定、跨单元格漂移；该结构源于投影偏置与RoPE引起的散射两种独立机制。据此提出VAR-Q：沿相同两轴分组，利用生成循环天然提供的块边界，免离群检测与校准，并结合位打包内核实现运行时内存压缩。

Result: 在涵盖两种范式的9个模型上实现低比特量化，KV内存最多减少78.5%，质量退化可忽略，并支持6倍更大的推理批大小。

Conclusion: 视觉自回归KV缓存具有沿特征与块轴向组织的离群结构，据此进行轴向分组量化无需校准即可有效压缩缓存，为大规模视觉生成部署提供了实用的内存优化方案。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive generation spans two dominant paradigms: next-scale prediction for images and video, and next-frame prediction for video. Both decode block-by-block rather than token-by-token, yielding KV caches whose statistics diverge from those of language models. At scale, KV memory exceeds the weights and limits large-scale deployment caused by a large amount of visual tokens. Our analysis of these caches reveals three findings. First, a small set of massive-value channels carries the functional load: ablating these outlier channels collapses generation, whereas ablating a matched set of inlier channels leaves quality intact. Second, the outliers organize along two axes, feature and block, with magnitudes persistent within each cell and shifting across cells. Third, this structure arises from two independent mechanisms: projection bias and RoPE-induced scattering. Guided by these findings, we propose VAR-Q, a tuning-free KV-cache quantization framework that groups along the same two axes. The generation loop provides block boundaries for free, removing the need for outlier detection or calibration. Paired with bit-packing kernels for runtime memory reduction, VAR-Q delivers low-bit quantization across nine models spanning both paradigms, cutting KV memory by up to 78.5% with negligible quality degradation and enabling 6x larger inference batches.

</details>


### [2] [Visual Forensic Detection of Fabricated Bangla News Photocards using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05403)
*Anindya Kundu,Kabya Mithun Saha,Md. Muhaiminul Islam,Fayek Ahmed,Aminul Kader Bulbul,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将孟加拉语"图文卡片"(photocard)假新闻检测重构为视觉图像伪造检测任务，构建了含969张真实、1077张伪造卡片的数据集（经16种光度/形态变换扩充至32,736样本），并比较了ANN、紧凑CNN与预训练YOLOv8n-cls三种模型的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在孟加拉国，大量本地化虚假信息以photocard形式传播——即模仿知名新闻机构品牌标识的单图新闻摘要。由于误导信息存在于像素层面而非机器可读文本中，基于文本的事实核查工具无法识别，因此需要从视觉伪造角度切入检测。

Method: 从公开Facebook页面抓取数据集：真实卡片来自17家国家级电视广播机构的认证页面，伪造卡片来自伪广播机构页面、匿名主页和公开群组。对每张图施加16种光度与形态变换扩充语料。对比三种模型：(1)全连接ANN；(2)紧凑型全批归一化四块CNN（489,954参数，比ANN小约1,644倍）；(3)ImageNet预训练的YOLOv8n-cls骨干（1.44M参数）微调。采用无泄漏分层分组五折交叉验证（同一卡片的所有增强版本固定在同一折）。同时诊断并修复了未归一化分类头导致的一类崩溃问题，并进行了人工错误分析与偏差-方差诊断。

Result: ANN准确率仅约52%，损失停滞于ln(2)≈0.693，表明展平图像破坏了伪造检测所需的空间线索。紧凑CNN从头训练达到87.45%±0.53%，分类头由ANN热启动后达88.75%±1.55%。预训练YOLOv8n-cls在相同折上达到97.36%±0.42%，显示了紧凑模型之上仍存在的性能余量。论文还报告了各类别的精确率、召回率、F1及合并混淆矩阵。

Conclusion: photocard假新闻检测本质上依赖空间视觉线索而非文本或扁平像素特征；紧凑CNN在无泄漏协议下具备有效但尚未饱和的性能，预训练骨干揭示了显著的性能上限空间；论文同时指出紧凑CNN存在欠拟合问题，并给出了一类崩溃问题的成因与修复方案。

Abstract: In Bangladesh, much localized misinformation spreads as photocards: single-image news summaries that imitate the branding of well-known news organizations. Because the misleading message lives in the pixels rather than in machine-readable text, text-based fact-checking tools cannot see it. We recast photocard fake-news detection as visual image forgery detection. We build a dataset of 969 authentic and 1,077 fabricated Bangla photocards scraped from public Facebook pages: authentic cards from the verified pages of seventeen national television broadcasters, fabricated ones from pseudo-broadcaster pages, anonymous profiles, and public groups. Sixteen photometric and morphological transformations per image grow the corpus to 32,736 samples. A fully connected ANN reaches only about 52% accuracy, its loss stuck at ln(2) ~ 0.693, showing that flattening the image destroys the spatial cues forgery detection needs. A compact, fully batch-normalized four-block CNN with 489,954 parameters, about 1,644x smaller than the ANN, is evaluated with leakage-free stratified group five-fold cross-validation, which keeps every augmented version of a card in one fold. Under this protocol the CNN reaches 87.45% +/- 0.53% pooled accuracy from scratch and 88.75% +/- 1.55% when its classifier head is warm-started from the ANN. An ImageNet-pretrained YOLOv8n-cls backbone (1.44M parameters) fine-tuned on the same folds reaches 97.36% +/- 0.42%, marking the headroom above the compact model. We report per-class precision, recall, F1, and pooled confusion matrices, diagnose and fix a one-class collapse caused by an unnormalized classifier head, and give a manual error analysis and bias-variance diagnosis showing that the compact CNNs under-fit.

</details>


### [3] [mmHand: Toward Pixel-Level-Accuracy Hand Localization Using a Single Commodity mmWave Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05404)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Zhengxiong Li,Chenhan Xu,Luchuan Song,Huining Li,Hongfei Xue,Yingxiao Wu,Wenyao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出mmHand——一种基于单台商用毫米波设备的非穿戴式、隐私保护的动态手部定位系统，通过毫米波数据图像化与跨模态空间特征增强模型，实现像素级精度的手部定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部定位应用广泛，但现有技术受数据模态限制存在不足：基于视觉的方法引发隐私问题，基于穿戴设备的方法牺牲用户舒适度。因此需要一种非接触、隐私保护且高精度的定位方案。

Method: 1) 提出mmImage生成工具，从原始毫米波数据中充分提取空间信息，构建新颖的二维图像格式表示；2) 设计框架，提供新的质量评估方法和毫米波数据的像素空间标注；3) 提出跨模态空间特征增强模型，具备强空间特征提取能力，在毫米波雷达U-V像素坐标系中实现像素级手部定位。

Result: 在三种场景、12名受试者上开展实验，四项指标结果均验证了该系统手部定位的有效性。

Conclusion: mmHand证明了单台商用毫米波设备可实现非穿戴、隐私保护的像素级动态手部定位，为手部定位问题提供了可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The hand localization problem has been a longstanding focus due to its many applications. The task involves modeling the hand as a singular point and determining its position within a defined coordinate system. However, due to data modality limitations, existing hand localization technologies face several challenges. For example, vision-based localization raises privacy concerns, while wearable-based methods compromise user comfort. In this paper, we introduce mmHand, a new device-free, privacy-preserving dynamic hand localization system with pixel-level accuracy, using a single commodity mmWave device. We first propose a mmImage generation tool to fully extract spatial information from raw mmWave data and introduce a novel 2D image-format representation of mmWave data. Next, we design a framework that provides a new quality evaluation method and pixel space labeling for the mmWave data. Finally, we present a cross-modality spatial feature-enhanced model with high spatial feature extraction capabilities, which can accurately localize hand positions at the pixel level in the mmWave radar U-V pixel coordinate system. We evaluate the system with experiments on 12 subjects in three scenarios, and the results across four metrics demonstrate the effectiveness of our hand localization system.

</details>


### [4] [When Does Sharing Work? Scaling a Shared SigLIP2 Across Image, Video, Audio, and Audio-Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05406)
*Xiaofei Sun,Peng Wang,Huayao Liu,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Song,Deqing Li,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Omni-Encoder：用单个全共享参数的 SigLIP-2 ViT 统一编码图像、视频、音频与音视频，通过模态原生输入、时间戳参数化的多模态 RoPE 与交替模态训练实现端到端联合优化，在四模态 16 个基准上取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比预训练中图像、视频、音频编码器仍独立训练，跨模态对齐主要依赖将各模态事后绑定到冻结的锚点空间（anchor-centric binding）或加装逐模态适配器，参数冗余且对齐不彻底；作者希望以单套共享参数实现真正的联合多模态编码。

Method: 1) 架构：单一 SigLIP-2 ViT，图像/视频/音频/音视频全共享参数；2) 模态原生输入阶段：将各信号映射到共享嵌入空间，其中 1D 卷积音频分词器把 mel 频谱转换为与视觉 patch 对齐的 token；3) 多模态旋转位置编码：以绝对时间戳为参数，将视觉帧与音频片段置于统一时空坐标系；4) 交替模态训练：图像、视频、音频、时间对齐音视频批次交替输入，梯度累积为一次更新。

Result: 以一套共享参数在四种模态共 16 个基准上取得有竞争力表现；联合训练相较各模态单独训练的对应模型，在每个模态上均有提升。

Conclusion: 验证了无需锚点绑定或逐模态适配器、以完全共享参数端到端联合训练即可统一多模态编码，且模态间存在正向迁移；交替模态梯度累积是稳定联合优化的关键。代码与模型将公开。

Abstract: Contrastive pretraining has advanced image-text understanding, yet image, video, and audio encoders are still trained independently, with cross-modal alignment realized mainly through post-hoc binding to a frozen anchor space. We present Omni-Encoder, a general multimodal encoder that processes image, video, audio, and audio-visual inputs within a single SigLIP-2 Vision Transformer with fully shared parameters. Unlike anchor-centric binding or per-modality adapters, all modalities are optimized jointly end-to-end, and the framework is organized from input to training. A modality-native input stage first maps each signal into the shared embedding space, including a 1D-convolutional audio tokenizer that converts mel-spectrograms into tokens aligned with visual patches. A multimodal rotary position embedding parameterized by absolute timestamps then places visual frames and audio segments in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Finally, alternating-modality training interleaves image, video, audio, and temporally aligned audio-visual batches and accumulates their gradients into one update, which we find central to stable joint optimization. Omni-Encoder achieves competitive results on 16 benchmarks across all four modalities with one shared parameter set, and joint training improves each modality over its separately trained counterpart. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [5] [MvBody: Multi-View-Based Hybrid Transformer Using Optical 3D Body Scan for Explainable Cesarean Section Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03212)
*Ruting Cheng,Boyuan Feng,Yijiang Zheng,Chuhui Qiu,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Joaquin A. Calderon,Wentao Zhao,Qing Pan,James K. Hahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MvBody——一种基于多视角3D人体扫描的Transformer网络，在孕31–38周仅用自报医疗数据和3D光学体形扫描预测剖宫产风险，独立测试集准确率84.62%、AUC-ROC 0.724，并用Integrated Gradients提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗资源有限地区难以获得院内分娩期参数，现有剖宫产风险预测模型多依赖产程期间的院内指标，无法用于基层或居家场景；作者希望验证用低成本通用设备可采集的3D体形信息进行早期风险评估的可行性。

Method: 构建多视角Transformer网络MvBody，融合孕31–38周的自报医疗数据与3D光学体形扫描；为解决数据稀缺下的训练效率与泛化性问题，引入度量学习损失；与主流机器学习模型及先进3D分析方法对比，并采用Integrated Gradients进行理论支撑的归因解释。

Result: 独立测试集上准确率84.62%、AUC-ROC 0.724，优于对比的机器学习模型和最新3D分析方法；解释性分析显示孕前体重、产妇年龄、产科史、既往剖宫产史以及头肩部位体形是剖宫产风险的关键贡献因素。

Conclusion: 3D体形结合少量自报信息即可实现可行的剖宫产风险早期预测，为资源受限环境下借助平价通用设备开展产前风险评估提供了初步依据；作为试点研究，样本与AUC水平仍有限，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Accurately assessing the risk of cesarean section (CS) delivery is critical, especially in settings with limited medical resources, where access to healthcare is often restricted. Early and reliable risk prediction allows better-informed prenatal care decisions and can improve maternal and neonatal outcomes. However, most existing predictive models are tailored for in-hospital use during labor and rely on parameters that are often unavailable in resource-limited or home-based settings. In this study, we conduct a pilot investigation to examine the feasibility of using 3D body shape for CS risk assessment for future applications with more affordable general devices. We propose a novel multi-view-based Transformer network, MvBody, which predicts CS risk using only self-reported medical data and 3D optical body scans obtained between the 31st and 38th weeks of gestation. To enhance training efficiency and model generalizability in data-scarce environments, we incorporate a metric learning loss into the network. Compared to widely used machine learning models and the latest advanced 3D analysis methods, our method demonstrates superior performance, achieving an accuracy of 84.62% and an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUC-ROC) of 0.724 on the independent test set. To improve transparency and trust in the model's predictions, we apply the Integrated Gradients algorithm to provide theoretically grounded explanations of the model's decision-making process. Our results indicate that pre-pregnancy weight, maternal age, obstetric history, previous CS history, and body shape, particularly around the head and shoulders, are key contributors to CS risk prediction.

</details>


### [6] [PEEK: One-Step-Look-Ahead Exposure Bias Correction for Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05407)
*Yunwei Bai,Ying Kiat Tan,Tsuhan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练确定性采样器插件PEEK，通过前瞻一步评估去噪器并插值来缓解采样中的曝光偏差，在8个扩散模型上显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样时，去噪网络基于与真实数据分布差异较大的有偏输入进行预测，存在训练-测试差距（曝光偏差），导致生成图像模糊、细节缺失或形状异常，限制了采样器与去噪网络之间的匹配度。

Method: PEEK在每步先做一次临时单步更新得到前瞻状态，在该状态上评估去噪器，并与“再下一步”的预测进行插值，形成下一个噪声状态；利用来自更高信号噪声状态的更接近真实图像的预测来缓解偏差。插值位置通过插值调度搜索优化，并用前缀缓存与基线噪声调度重定基缓存提升效率。

Result: 在8个像素空间与隐空间扩散模型（无条件、标签引导、文本引导设置，多种分辨率）上，PEEK在NFE匹配基线下持续提升，FID最高改善49.3%，额外计算开销极小（校准耗时最多约2.2分钟）。

Conclusion: PEEK作为即插即用的免训练确定性采样插件，能有效缓解曝光偏差并以极低开销提升多类扩散模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion image generators are successful due to accurate denoising networks and strong numerical samplers. Yet the two components are not fully matched. During sampling, the denoiser predicts for the next step based on flawed input that differ much from the real data. This train-test gap, or exposure bias, caps image generation quality (i.e., blurry, lacking in details or awkward in shape). To mitigate the problem, we propose PEEK, a novel training-free deterministic sampler plugin. At each eligible denoising step, PEEK first performs a provisional one-step update, evaluates the denoiser once at the look-ahead state, and interpolates it with the one-step-later prediction to form the next noisy state. By taking the additional step, we obtain a generally more-image-like prediction from the higher-signal noisy state, mitigating the exposure bias. To optimize the interpolation positions, PEEK searches for an interpolation schedule search made efficient by the prefix and baseline noise-schedule-rebasing caches. On 8 pixel-space and latent-space diffusion models with unconditional, label-guided, and text-guided settings across various output resolutions, PEEK consistently improves over NFE-matched baselines, with FID improvements up to 49.3\% at minimal extra computation cost (e.g., the one-time-per-model calibration takes at most 2.2 minutes across our experiments.). Project website is at https://dachengruoque.github.io/PEEK-Video/.

</details>


### [7] [Chronos: Sequential Identity-Preserving Video Generation by Temporal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05893)
*Mengqi Huang,Wenchuan Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yijing Tu,Zhike Liu,Luoni Wang,Ning Tong,Xiaoxiao Sheng,Tianyu Huang,Xuecheng Qi,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Chronos 框架，将单段人脸身份保持与多段序列动作生成统一到时序建模范式下，通过语义提示锚定、视觉帧锚定与度量引导候选路由三模块缓解身份保真与动作可控性的冲突，并在 ACM MM 2026 身份保持视频生成挑战赛 Track 1 与 Track 2 均获第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖参考图引导控制信号与身份感知条件以提升人脸对齐，但仅强化静态身份对齐会在身份保真与时间可控性之间形成根本性张力，在大姿态变化与多段连续动作场景下易出现身份漂移或动作抑制。

Method: Chronos 将单段人脸身份保持与多段序列动作生成统一到同一时序建模框架中，包含三部分：Semantic Prompt Anchoring（自适应精炼用户提示词）、Visual Frame Anchoring（将条件转化为视觉落地的关键帧锚点以提供视觉引导）、Metric-Guided Candidate Routing（通过多维评估从异构生成器中筛选最高质量视频）。

Result: 大量实验验证了方法的优越性，在 ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge 的 Track 1 与 Track 2 中均取得第一名，在人脸身份保持与序列动作生成两类设定下均展现有效性。

Conclusion: 以统一的时序形式同时刻画身份保持与序列动作控制，并辅以提示精炼、关键帧锚定与多生成器候选路由，可有效平衡身份保真度与时间可控性，且在竞赛级评测中达到领先水平。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to synthesize high-quality and visually realistic videos from textual and visual conditions while faithfully maintaining the subject's identity across diverse motions. Prior methods have extensively introduced reference-guided control signals and identity-aware conditioning to improve facial alignment from reference images. However, improving static identity alignment alone creates a fundamental tension between identity fidelity and temporal controllability, which frequently causes identity drift or motion suppression under large pose variations and multi-segment sequential actions. To address this, we propose Chronos, a framework that unifies single-segment facial identity preservation and multi-segment sequential action generation under a common temporal formulation. Specifically, Chronos integrates Semantic Prompt Anchoring to adaptively refine the user prompt, Visual Frame Anchoring to transform conditions into visually grounded keyframe anchors for visual guidance, and Metric-Guided Candidate Routing to select the highest-quality videos from heterogeneous generators via multi-dimensional evaluation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach, which achieved first place in both Track 1 and Track 2 of the ACM MM 2026 Identity-Preserving Video Generation Challenge, demonstrating its effectiveness across both facial identity preservation and sequential action generation settings.

</details>


### [8] [Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation for Low-textured Scientific Sketch Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05408)
*Zhiqiang Wang,Sheri L. Barton,Jennifer H. Johnson,Yi He,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation (ART)，把数据增强选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，自适应构造增强视图库后用于自监督微调，提升低纹理草图图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低纹理草图图像（如野生动物保护、考古绘图、法医合成画像）缺乏现代深度学习模型所依赖的细节纹理和视觉变化，表示学习与检索困难；且草图对旋转、翻转、缩放、拉伸等变换高度敏感，随机增强可能引入破坏结构完整性的失真。

Method: 将增强选择建模为多臂老虎机（MAB）问题，把各类变换视为arm，通过奖励持续评估其有效性，在自监督微调前自适应构造奖励引导的视图库；后续的表示损失不回传至ART，即ART与微调解耦。

Result: 在Mote Marine Laboratory提供的Florida manatee草图上，变换疤痕检索平均Top-1准确率71.40%（随机增强选择为68.83%）；位置感知草图检索55.84%（冻结CLIP为18.18%）。

Conclusion: 奖励引导的增强选择优于随机增强，能显著提升草图检索性能，表明对变换敏感的草图数据应自适应而非随机地选择增强策略。

Abstract: Low-textured sketch images are widely used in scientific applications, including wildlife conservation, archaeological illustration, and forensic composites. Learning from and retrieving such images is challenging because they lack detailed surface textures and visual variations commonly exploited by modern deep learning models. This paper proposes Augmentation with Reinforcement Transformation (ART) to fine-tune pretrained models to learn effective representations for accurate sketch image retrieval. Our key observation is that sketches are particularly sensitive to image transformations, such as rotation, flipping, zooming, and stretching. While some transformations preserve their structural integrity, others may introduce distortions that hinder representation learning. Instead of randomly choosing transformations to construct fine-tuning views, ART formulates augmentation selection as a multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem, where transformations are treated as arms and their effectiveness is continuously evaluated through rewards. This enables ART to adaptively construct reward-guided view banks before self-supervised fine-tuning; the subsequent representation loss does not feed back to ART. On Florida manatee sketches provided by Mote Marine Laboratory, ART achieves 71.40% average Top-1 accuracy for transformed-scar retrieval, compared with 68.83% for random augmentation selection, and 55.84% for location-aware sketch retrieval, compared with 18.18% for frozen CLIP.

</details>


### [9] [Clips2Story: Training-Free Video Storyboarding and Editing Using Multimodal Retrieval-Embedded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05992)
*Junnuo Wang,Weihan Xu,Hao-Wen Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Clips2Story 是一个无需训练、基于 LLM 的流水线，可从无序片段池中按用户给定关键词检索相关片段，并由 LLM 挑选、排序并生成连接性旁白组成可编辑故事板，最终合成短片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑常面对大量碎片化素材，需要筛选并组织成连贯故事；但现有视频编辑与摘要方法假设源视频时间有序，并致力于保留原叙事与时间结构，难以直接适用于无序片段池的关键词驱动创作需求。

Method: 方法为 training-free 的 LLM 流水线：(1) 为每个片段提取基于文本的多模态描述符，并检索与关键词最相关的片段；(2) 将候选片段的文本描述输入 LLM，由其选择并排序，生成带连接性旁白（connective narrations）的可编辑故事板；(3) 拼接所选片段，并为旁白检索配套视觉素材，合成输出视频。评估上构建合成数据集：从 5 类题材的 100 个长视频出发，用场景变化检测模型切分为短片段并打乱以去除时间顺序。

Result: 客观评测与定性示例表明，Clips2Story 能有效检索关键词相关片段并基于关键词构建连贯故事板；人工评估显示其在叙事连贯性、关键词对齐度与整体真实性上优于强基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将片段池的关键词驱动短片生成建模为"检索—LLM 排序编排—素材合成"的无训练流程，验证了无需依赖原始时间结构即可产出连贯叙事短片的可行性。

Abstract: Video editors often work with large collections of fragmented footage and must select and organize relevant clips into a coherent story. However, many existing video editing and summarization methods assume a temporally ordered source video and aim to preserve its original narrative and temporal structure. In this paper, we introduce Clips2Story, a training-free, LLM-based pipeline that enables users to create keyword-specified short videos from a pool of video clips. Given a pool of clips and a user-specified keyword, we first extract text-based multimodal descriptors for each clip and retrieve the clips most relevant to the keyword. We then provide the textual descriptors of these candidate clips to an LLM, which selects and arranges them into an editable storyboard with connective narrations. Finally, we assemble the selected clips and retrieve accompanying visuals for the narrations to compose the output video. To evaluate the proposed pipeline, we construct a synthetic evaluation dataset from 100 long-form videos across five genres by segmenting each video into short clips using a scene-change detection model and shuffling the resulting clips to remove their temporal order. Through objective evaluation and qualitative examples, we demonstrate that Clips2Story can effectively retrieve keyword-relevant clips and compose coherent storyboards based on user-specified keywords. Human evaluation further shows that Clips2Story outperforms a strong baseline in terms of narrative coherence, keyword alignment, and overall realism.

</details>


### [10] [PointNDF: Cascaded Neural Displacement Fields for Point Cloud Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05411)
*Michael Y. Wang,Pingping Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointNDF用PTv3编码全局块描述子，并通过神经位移场（NDF）融合每点邻域特征、以有界向量与尺度因子双分支预测位移，三阶段级联逐步精化点位置，实现更均匀的点分布与竞争力的去噪精度和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的点云去噪多采用"逐点特征提取+简单回归预测逐点位移"的两阶段流程，缺乏鲁棒的特征引导与分布感知的几何精化，难以兼顾点均匀性与几何质量。

Method: 提出PointNDF框架，结合块级几何上下文与局部位移预测：先用Point Transformer V3（PTv3）编码器将多尺度几何信息聚合为全局块描述子；再用神经位移场（NDF）将该全局描述子与逐点邻域特征融合，并通过有界向量分支与尺度因子分支预测位移；最后用三个级联NDF阶段传播局部特征并逐步精化点位置。

Result: 在PU-Net与ModelNet10数据集上取得具有竞争力的去噪精度和较好的推理效率，并开源代码（https://github.com/peridous/PointNDF）。

Conclusion: 以块级几何先验引导、约束型位移预测与多阶段级联精化的方式可同时改善点云去噪的几何保真度与点分布均匀性，是一种高效可行的去噪方案。

Abstract: Point cloud denoising aims to recover clean geometry from noisy 3D observations while preserving both fine surface details and the underlying spatial distribution of the points. Many learning-based methods adopt a two-stage pipeline that first extracts point-wise features from the noisy input and subsequently predicts point-wise displacements via simple regressions. However, these approaches often lack robust feature guidance and distribution-aware geometric refinement, causing them to fail at maintaining optimal point uniformity and geometric quality. To address these limitations, we propose PointNDF, a denoising framework that combines patch-level geometric context with local displacement prediction. Specifically, a Point Transformer V3 (PTv3) encoder aggregates multiscale geometric information into a global patch descriptor. Rather than directly regressing an unconstrained displacement vector, a Neural Displacement Field (NDF) fuses this global patch descriptor with per-point neighborhood features. It then predicts each displacement through bounded-vector and scale factor branches, thereby achieving a superior, more uniform point distribution. Three cascaded NDF stages propagate local features and progressively refine the point positions. Experiments on PU-Net and ModelNet10 demonstrate competitive denoising accuracy and favorable inference efficiency. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/peridous/PointNDF.

</details>


### [11] [Entropogram-guided Visual Realignment Decoding: Capturing the Rhythm of Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06415)
*Jiaqi Deng,Zhe Fu,Yakun Chen,Dichang Zhang,Zonghan Wu,Xiaoshui Huang,Huan Huo,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从推理生成动力学视角重新审视MLLM幻觉，发现两类熵模式与幻觉相关，并提出熵感知的自省式解码框架，在六个基准九个设置上提升推理准确性与抗幻觉鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在跨模态推理上进展显著，但长链推理过程中仍存在幻觉。作者希望从解码阶段的生成动力学（熵动态）角度理解并缓解幻觉，而非将熵仅视为静态不确定性度量。

Method: 系统性分析解码过程中的熵动态，归纳出两类幻觉相关熵模式：整体高置信区间对应过早收敛到不当的多模态交互；局部熵的突变对应推理不稳定与幻觉起始。基于此提出熵感知自省框架：解码中持续跟踪熵动态，检测到可疑幻觉状态时执行针对性视觉重对齐，促使模型在关键推理阶段重新审视显著视觉证据。

Result: 在六个多模态推理基准、九种评估设置下的广泛实验表明，方法在推理准确率和抗幻觉鲁棒性上均有稳健提升，并为MLLM失败背后的生成动力学提供新见解。

Conclusion: 熵可作为刻画多模态信息整合过程的结构化信号，基于熵动态的自省式重对齐是缓解MLLM长链推理幻觉的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress on cross-modal reasoning tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations in long-chain reasoning processes. In this work, we revisit hallucination in MLLMs from the perspective of reasoning generation dynamics. We argue that entropy is not merely a static measure of uncertainty but a structured signal that reflects how modality information is integrated throughout decoding. Through systematic analysis, we identify two characteristic entropy patterns associated with hallucination behaviors: (1) a generally high-confidence regime corresponds to premature convergence to inappropriate multimodal interactions; and (2) local abrupt entropy transitions are associated with reasoning instability and hallucination onset. Building on these observations, we propose an entropy-aware self-introspective framework for MLLM reasoning. Our method continuously tracks entropy dynamics during decoding and performs targeted visual re-alignment when suspicious hallucination states are detected, encouraging the model to re-examine salient visual evidence at critical reasoning stages. Extensive experiments across six multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning nine distinct evaluation settings demonstrate that our approach delivers robust improvements in reasoning accuracy and hallucination robustness, while providing new insights into the generation dynamics underlying MLLM failures. The code is available at: https://github.com/jden4524/SelfRetrospectiveDecoding.

</details>


### [12] [SurgTransfer: Multi-Step Latent Action Modeling for Action Transfer in Surgical World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05443)
*Saurabh Koju,Love Panta,Deepak Thapa,Rudra P. K. Poudel,Prajwal Ghimire,Angus Watson,Yash Raj Shrestha,Prashnna Kumar Gyawali,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多步动作学习框架，从无标注视频中学习上下文不变的潜在动作，以实现手术专业技能的跨情境迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术世界模型有潜力提供术中引导、加速无风险训练并促进专家手术技巧迁移，但该方向尚缺乏探索；现有方法依赖真实运动学动作或特定任务的专家标注，适用范围受限；而自然领域从无标注视频学习潜在动作的做法对手术域而言过于短视，无法刻画手术流程的层次化与目标导向结构。

Method: 多步动作学习框架（multi-step action learning framework），学习上下文不变的潜在动作（context-invariant latent actions），使其可跨不同手术情境迁移；仅依赖无标注视频。

Result: 在公开数据集 SurgToolLoc-2022 与 PitVis-2023 上进行大量实验，并通过定量指标与外科专家的定性评估验证方法有效性；代码与实现细节已开源。

Conclusion: 多步潜在动作学习能够克服单步范式的短视问题，捕捉手术流程的层次化、目标导向结构，产出可迁移的上下文不变动作表示，为手术世界模型中的技能迁移提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical world models have the potential to provide intra-operative guidance, accelerate risk-free training and facilitate transfer of expert surgical maneuvers. However, the use of world models for transferring surgical expertise has been largely unexplored. Moreover, recent approaches in this domain have mainly relied on ground truth kinematics actions or expert annotations for specific tasks, limiting their applicability beyond the narrow range of tasks with ground-truth annotations. In contrast, existing works in the natural domain have shown promising results in learning latent actions solely from unlabeled videos and transferring the learned actions across contexts. We observe that this paradigm is short-sighted for the surgical domain, failing to capture the hierarchical and goal-directed structure of surgical procedures. Thus, we propose a novel multi-step action learning framework to learn context-invariant latent actions that are easily transferable across various surgical contexts. We perform extensive experiments on the publicly available datasets, SurgToolLoc-2022 and PitVis-2023, verifying our approach with both quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation by surgical experts. We have published our code and the implementation details at https://github.com/bhattarailab/Surgical-Vision-World-Model.

</details>


### [13] [SO(3)-Equivariant Learning on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05449)
*Matteo Ballegeer,Dries F. Benoit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SO3brep：面向B-rep的SO(3)等变图神经网络，在本地坐标系计算特征并用Wigner D矩阵映射到共享坐标系，通过Clebsch-Gordan张量积保留相邻面的相对朝向，实现SE(3)不变输出，在旋转测试下精度最高且参数最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-rep学习需对零件姿态鲁棒，而CAD模型不以规范朝向存储。现有B-rep网络要么在世界坐标系下处理坐标与法向，旋转时性能退化；要么在每面局部坐标系编码，虽获不变性但面间消息退化为标量、丢失相邻面相对朝向信息。

Method: 提出SO3brep，在B-rep面邻接图上构建SO(3)等变图神经网络。节点特征在局部坐标系计算，经Wigner D矩阵映射至共享坐标系；用Clebsch-Gordan张量积将特征与相邻面间方向耦合，使相对朝向在整个消息传递中保持，输出保持SE(3)不变。

Result: 在三个面分割与两个分类基准（测试零件随机旋转）上，SO3brep在对比的B-rep网络和等变点云网络中精度最高，且参数量最少；在标注零件稀少（工业CAD典型场景）时优势最显著。

Conclusion: 通过SO(3)等变设计在B-rep学习中兼顾旋转不变性与相对朝向保留，以更少参数取得更优精度，尤其适合标注稀缺的工业CAD应用。

Abstract: Learning on boundary representations (B-reps) requires robustness to the pose of a part, since CAD models are not stored in a canonical orientation. Existing B-rep networks either operate on world-frame coordinates and normals, and thus degrade under rotation, or encode each face in a local reference frame. Local frames yield invariance, but reduce inter-face messages to scalars and discard the relative orientation of adjacent faces. We propose SO3brep, an SO(3)-equivariant graph neural network on the B-rep face-adjacency graph. Node features are computed in local frames and mapped into a shared frame by Wigner D-matrices. Clebsch-Gordan tensor products couple these features with the directions between adjacent faces, so that relative orientation is preserved throughout message passing and the output remains SE(3)-invariant. Across three face-segmentation and two classification benchmarks with randomly rotated test parts, SO3brep achieves the highest accuracy among the compared B-rep networks and equivariant point cloud networks, with the fewest parameters. Its advantage is largest with few labeled parts, a setting typical of industrial CAD. Code is available at https://github.com/UGent-CVAMO/so3brep.

</details>


### [14] [From Tokens to Trajectories: Parameter-Efficient Continuous Adaptation of Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05451)
*Gayatri Deshmukh,Onkar Susladkar,Adheesh Juvekar,Halil Kilicoglu,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowShift 是一个参数高效的后训练框架，通过将预训练的离散 UMM 转换为连续 rectified-flow 生成器，绕过离散 tokenization 带来的量化瓶颈，在保留语义先验的同时显著提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）采用离散 tokenization 虽具备强语义推理能力，但引入严格的量化瓶颈，损害细粒度视觉细节，导致生成图像结构正确却伪影严重。因此需要在不丢弃骨干语义先验的前提下提升视觉质量。

Method: FlowShift 用连续潜变量投影替换离散视觉接口，并对注意力层应用 LoRA 微调；同时提出双目标速度向量蒸馏（DOVD），将模型对齐到高保真连续教师模型与真实连续轨迹，从而在避免语义漂移的同时提升感知质量。

Result: 在主流离散 UMM 上应用后，COCO-30K 的 FID 与美学评分显著提升，同时保留 99.5% 以上的多模态理解能力；消融实验证实 DOVD 是生成质量提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: FlowShift 实现了高保真连续生成，且不牺牲预训练的语义 grounding，为离散 UMM 向连续生成的迁移提供了高效后训练范式。

Abstract: While discrete tokenization in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) establishes strong semantic reasoning, it introduces a strict quantization bottleneck that degrades fine-grained visual details, often resulting in structurally accurate but artifact-heavy images. We introduce FlowShift, a parameter-efficient post-training framework that bypasses this bottleneck by converting pre-trained discrete UMMs into continuous rectified-flow generators. Instead of discarding the backbone's semantic priors, FlowShift replaces discrete visual interfaces with continuous latent projections and applies LoRA to the attention layers. To efficiently boost visual quality without semantic drift, we propose Dual-Objective Velocity Vector Distillation (DOVD), which aligns the model with a high-fidelity continuous teacher and ground-truth continuous trajectories. This approach drastically improves perceptual quality while preserving prompt adherence and reasoning. When applied to leading discrete UMMs, FlowShift significantly improves COCO-30K FID and aesthetic scores, while retaining over 99.5% of multimodal understanding capabilities. Ablations confirm DOVD drives generation quality. Ultimately, FlowShift achieves high-fidelity continuous generation without sacrificing pre-trained semantic grounding.

</details>


### [15] [From Activation to Intervention: Interpreting Sparse Visual Features through Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05522)
*Jianyang Gu,Jacob Beattie,Mridul Khurana,Julia Chae,Xi Xiao,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Sara Beery,Tanya Berger-Wolf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RecSAEI框架，通过沿SAE解码器方向干预表示并重建视觉输出，为视觉特征语义提供直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征解释依赖最强激活的图像或像素区域，但当不同语义特征（如同位置的纹理与颜色）频繁共现时，该证据产生歧义，需要更直接的验证信号。

Method: 将特征解释从被动观察转为主动视觉重建：沿SAE解码器方向对表示进行干预并重建输出，观测干预引起的可视变化以判定特征语义；并将[CLS]的SAE干预归因到稀疏的patch SAE解码器方向集合上。

Result: 在多个数据集与多种视觉编码器上成功消解特征语义歧义；[CLS]干预归因不仅带来稳定的目标响应，还揭示了基本视觉模式与高层语义之间的组合关系。

Conclusion: 干预重建应作为解释神经网络的重要互补信号，与基于激活的观察性证据结合使用。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose visual representations into individual features, offering a promising basis for explaining model behavior. Interpreting these features typically relies on images or pixel regions that most strongly activate them. However, this evidence becomes ambiguous when multiple features with different semantics frequently activate in the same locations, such as texture and color in the same patterns. We propose Reconstructive SAE Intervention (RecSAEI), a framework that shifts feature explanation from passive observation to active visual reconstruction. By intervening on representations along SAE decoder directions and reconstructing the visual output, we provide direct evidence of a feature's semantic meaning. We demonstrate that our method successfully disambiguates feature meanings across multiple datasets and vision encoders. Furthermore, our framework grounds [CLS] SAE interventions in a sparse set of patch SAE decoder directions. Beyond stable target response, this attribution also exposes the compositional relationship between elementary visual patterns and high-level semantics. Our results advocate for intervention reconstruction as an essential complementary signal for explaining neural networks.

</details>


### [16] [How Finely Can Vision-Language Models See Biology? Bio-VQA: A Benchmark for Taxonomic, Morphological, Spatial and Individual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05559)
*S M Rayeed,Qian Ma,Charles V. Stewart,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出Bio-VQA，迄今最大的生物学生成式视觉问答基准（1.86M问题、217K图像、11个数据集），覆盖分类阶元推理、细粒度属性定位、定量空间推理与个体重识别四个维度，并将对错评分扩展为分级评分；评测18个开放权重模型发现通用视觉能力难以迁移到生物域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物学视觉基准多局限于少数类群的物种识别，无法检验VLM在"哪个类群、多少个体、何种性状、是否同一只个体"等多重问题上的能力，因此需要覆盖多维度生物视觉理解的统一评测。

Method: 构建Bio-VQA基准：整合11个公开数据集，含217K图像、1.86M问题，覆盖四个层面（层级分类阶元推理、细粒度属性定位、定量空间计数/尺寸测量、个体重识别）；引入分级评分（near-miss得分高于远错），评测18个3.75B–38.4B参数的开放权重模型，并与BioCLIP-2等域内专家对比。

Result: 通用视觉能力未有效迁移：(1)计数——在含2–10个个体的图像上最佳模型仅41.7%完全正确，不及同计数日常物体的一半；(2)分类——准确率由门级0.72降至种名0.12，近1/6，规模扩展主要改善粗阶元；(3)与专家差距随粒度增大，class级0.731 vs BioCLIP-2 0.874（1.2倍），种名级0.269 vs 0.544（2倍）；(4)性状尺寸测量上18个模型中15个不优于盲基线（对每个样本预测固定数值）；(5)重识别上18个中17个在对抗条件下低于随机水平；上述失败在模型规模十倍范围内持续存在。

Conclusion: Bio-VQA可作为检验VLM能力向生物域迁移程度的标准测试，揭示通用视觉模型在细粒度生物学任务上的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: In biodiversity monitoring, a single image raises several questions: which taxon, how many, which trait, and whether the individual has been seen before. Vision-language models (VLMs) could answer all of these from the image, but their ability is largely untested in biology, as existing benchmarks focus mostly on species identification within a few taxonomic groups. We present Bio-VQA, the largest biological visual question answering (VQA) benchmark and the only one to span four aspects of biological visual understanding: hierarchical taxonomic reasoning, fine-grained attribute grounding, quantitative spatial reasoning, and individual re-identification. Bio-VQA contains 1.86M questions over 217K images from 11 public datasets, and extends right-or-wrong scoring to graded scoring, so a near-miss counts for more than a distant one. Evaluating 18 open-weight models from 3.75B to 38.4B parameters, we find that general visual capabilities do not transfer to biological organisms: the best model counts animals exactly on 41.7% of images with 2 to 10 individuals, less than half its accuracy on everyday objects at the same counts. Taxonomic accuracy falls to one sixth, from 0.72 at phylum to 0.12 at scientific name, and scaling helps mostly at coarse ranks. The gap to a domain specialist widens accordingly, from 0.731 against BioCLIP-2's 0.874 at class (1.2x) to 0.269 against 0.544 at scientific name (2x). On trait size measurement, 15 of the 18 models do no better than an image-blind baseline that predicts one fixed number for every specimen, and on re-identification, 17 of the 18 models fall below chance under adversarial conditions. These failures persist across a tenfold range of model size. We hope Bio-VQA becomes a standard test of how well VLM capabilities transfer to the biological domain.

</details>


### [17] [Lesion Overlap Ranks PET Segmentation Models but Does Not Certify Patient-Level Tumor Burden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05564)
*Tushar Kataria,Gerald Sabin,Ponnuswamy Sadayappan,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在1,014例autoPET FDG PET/CT及597例PSMA研究中，模型平均Dice排序与平均MTV相对误差排序一致(ρ=-1.00)，但患者层面Dice与MTV误差阈值分类在16.8%患者上相悖，说明重叠指标无法保证个体MTV估计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTV用于全身PET疾病负荷的预后分层与疗效评估，其自动化估计只有在个体患者层面可靠时才有价值；然而分割模型通常用Dice等重叠指标评估，而非负荷误差。作者旨在检验重叠指标能否识别不可靠的MTV估计。

Method: 基于1,014例autoPET FDG PET/CT，评估未参与微调的LesionTracer预测；在489例肿瘤阳性患者上比较五个已发布模型；并在597例PSMA研究中验证。分析维度包括模型层面排序一致性、患者层面Dice与相对MTV误差的相关性、阈值分歧、假阳性与假阴性体积抵消效应，以及漏检病灶计数。

Result: 五个FDG模型中，按平均Dice排序与按平均相对MTV误差排序完全一致(ρ=-1.00, exact p=0.017)；患者层面折外Dice与相对MTV误差强相关(ρ=-0.75)，但489例中82例(16.8%)在Dice 0.80与MTV误差25%阈值上落入相反侧；训练暴露模型的相关性为-0.47至-0.74且处于较窄的高Dice区间；折外情形下，相反方向的假阳性与假阴性体积抵消了约三分之一的空间误差；489例中236例存在五模型均至少漏检一个病灶（未必为同一病灶）的情况。

Conclusion: 平均模型排序的一致性并不能确立个体MTV估计的可靠性，也无法揭示共有的漏检病灶。PET分割基准应在Dice等重叠指标之外，同时报告患者层面的MTV误差与漏检病灶数。

Abstract: Metabolic tumor volume (MTV) summarizes whole-body PET disease burden for prognostic stratification and treatment assessment. Automated MTV is useful only if its estimate is reliable for the individual patient, yet segmentation models are usually evaluated with overlap metrics such as Dice rather than burden error. We examined whether overlap metrics identify unreliable MTV estimates using 1,014 autoPET FDG PET/CT studies. We evaluated LesionTracer predictions held out from fine-tuning, compared five selected released models on 489 tumor-positive patients, and examined the findings in 597 PSMA studies. Across the five FDG models, ranking by higher mean Dice gave the same order as ranking by lower mean relative MTV error ($ρ=-1.00$, exact $p=0.017$). At the patient level, however, out-of-fold Dice tracked relative MTV error strongly ($ρ=-0.75$), yet 82 of 489 patients (16.8\%) fell on opposite sides of the Dice 0.80 and MTV-error 25\% thresholds. Within the training-exposed released models, correlations ranged from $-0.47$ to $-0.74$ over narrower, high-Dice ranges. Out of fold, opposing false-positive and false-negative volumes cancelled about one third of spatial error in net MTV. In 236 of 489 patients, each of the five models missed at least one lesion, though not necessarily the same lesion. Thus, agreement in average model rankings did not establish the reliability of individual MTV estimates or reveal shared lesion misses. PET segmentation benchmarks should report patient-level MTV error and missed-lesion counts alongside Dice and other overlap metrics.

</details>


### [18] [Discarded or Unreachable? Diagnosing and Repairing Spatial Relation Failure in Vision--Language Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05565)
*Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Zirui Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对比视觉-语言模型在左右关系上近乎随机，但其症状有两类病因（"访问失败"与"池化失败"），二者均位于稠密特征下游，故用一个零参数、查询条件的空间矩读出即可同时修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比视觉-语言模型对"左/右"空间关系的判别接近随机水平。作者指出该症状存在两种病因，且需要相反的修复方式：其一，关系信息仍保留在池化嵌入中，但原生图文相似度无法访问（access failure）；其二，池化丢弃了关系，而稠密特征仍保留（pooling failure）。因此需要一个能同时覆盖两者的统一读出机制。

Method: 在两个受控设定上诊断：(1) 干净的双物体照片，十个编码器，检验池化向量翻转奇分量中横向极性的线性可分性，并在百万级标题上做原生打分搜索；(2) 受控 C4 轨道（仅关系变化），比较池化与 patch 网格对排列轴及其极性的保留。据此提出零参数、查询条件的空间矩读出（spatial moment readout）：从 patch 网格重算几何，作为向量与原生嵌入并列存储。

Result: 双物体照片上，横向极性从池化翻转奇分量线性解码准确率 0.77–0.99，但百万标题搜索下十个编码器中有九个在原生打分上仍处随机（访问失败）；C4 轨道上池化保留排列轴 0.998–1.000 却丢失极性，而 patch 网格保留极性（池化失败）。在 118,287 图像索引上，对反向几何负例的左右成对准确率由 0.51–0.53 提升至 0.81–0.96；21 个同类候选中的条件 R@1 由 0.05 提升至 0.37–0.75；每批查询低于 1 毫秒；对不含关系的查询精确复现原生排序。

Conclusion: 左右关系失败并非单一原因，而是"访问失败"与"池化失败"两种可分离的机制；二者均位于稠密特征下游，因此单一零参数空间矩读出即可同时修复，且不损害原生检索行为。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models score left/right relations near chance, but the symptom has two causes that call for opposite repairs: the relation may survive in the pooled embedding yet be unreachable by native image-text similarity, or pooling may discard it while the dense features retain it. On clean two object photographs, across ten encoders, the lateral polarity is linearly decodable from the flip odd component of the pooled vector at 0.77-0.99, yet a search over a million captions leaves nine of the ten at chance under native scoring: an access failure. On controlled C4 orbits, where the relation is the only thing that varies, pooling keeps the arrangement axis at 0.998-1.000 and loses its polarity, which the patch grid retains: a pooling failure. Both sit downstream of the dense features, so one readout covers both. Our zero parameter, query conditioned spatial moment recomputes the geometry from the patch grid and stores it as a vector beside the native embedding. On a 118,287 image index it raises left/right pairwise accuracy against reversed geometry negatives from 0.51-0.53 to 0.81-0.96 and conditional R@1 among 21 category matched candidates from 0.05 to 0.37-0.75, at under a millisecond per batched query, while reproducing the native ranking exactly for queries that name no relation.

</details>


### [19] [Role-based Effectiveness of an Epistemic Uncertainty Estimate in Training Models for Semantic Segmentation of 3D LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05569)
*Krishnakumar N,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文显式建模点云分割中的偶然(认知)不确定性，并将其作为输入特征或逐样本损失权重两种角色使用，在四个大规模LiDAR数据集上验证其既能提升mIoU、恢复被基线完全漏检的少数类，又能在半数配置下改善误差与不确定性的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云固有的稀疏性、噪声与无序性使可靠分类困难。现有SOTA多依赖训练模型显式建模不确定性，但缺乏对"生成式方法估计的不确定性可扮演何种角色"的系统探索，即不确定性是否仅用于提升精度。

Method: 显式建模epistemic不确定性，构造两种不确定性感知变体：(1) 将不确定性作为额外输入特征；(2) 将每样本置信度权重嵌入训练损失。在四个大规模LiDAR基准（ISPRS Vaihingen、AHN3、Paris-Lille-3D、Toronto-3D，覆盖机载与移动采集）上，配合PointNet++与RandLA-Net参考模型及其不确定性增强版本，采用规范的空间不相交训练/测试划分进行评测，并以Patch Accuracy vs Patch Uncertainty衡量误差与不确定性的对齐。

Result: 在空间不相交协议下，不确定性感知建模提升了mIoU，恢复了基线ANN完全无法预测的少数类，并在半数配置中提高了预测误差与估计不确定性的一致性。

Conclusion: 不确定性在分割流程中可承担多重角色，而非仅作为提升精度的工具；应将其视为特征或损失加权等信息通道加以利用。

Abstract: Point cloud segmentation is a fundamental task in 3D data analysis, with applications across robotics, augmented and virtual reality, and remote sensing. However, the inherent sparsity, noise, and unordered nature of point clouds introduce significant challenges, particularly in achieving reliable classification. While the state-of-the-art rely on training models for explicit modeling of uncertainty, there is a gap in exploring different roles that estimated uncertainty using generative approaches can play. This work investigates the impact of deterministic uncertainty in point cloud segmentation by explicitly modeling epistemic uncertainty and using it in different roles, namely, as a feature and in loss function. We evaluate a baseline artificial neural network (ANN) and two uncertainty-aware variants (uncertainty as an additional input feature and as a per-sample confidence weight in the training loss) on four large-scale LiDAR benchmarks spanning airborne and mobile acquisition, namely ISPRS Vaihingen, AHN3, Paris-Lille-3D, and Toronto-3D, together with PointNet++ and RandLA-Net references and their uncertainty-augmented counterparts under canonical spatially disjoint train and test splits. Under this protocol, uncertainty-aware modeling improves mean Intersection over Union, recovers minority classes that the baseline ANN never predicts at all, and in half of the configurations improves the alignment between prediction error and estimated uncertainty as measured by Patch Accuracy versus Patch Uncertainty. Our findings indicate that uncertainty can play multiple roles in the segmentation pipeline rather than as a device for improving accuracy alone.

</details>


### [20] [ChangeJEPA: Predict Before You Compare for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05570)
*Buddhi Wijenayake,Dhananjaya Jayasundara,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChangeJEPA将双时相变化检测重构为"潜在后状态预测"：用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在潜空间预测变化后完整表示，并以多源不一致性推断变化，在三个基准上超过匹配的孪生解码器基线最多1.51 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统孪生特征比较框架中，两个编码器独立编码，既保留真实地表变化，也保留光照、季节、辐射与几何等 nuisance 变化，导致解码器难以区分有意义变化与瞬时效用差异。

Method: 提出ChangeJEPA：以变化前图像与部分变化后证据为输入，在潜空间预测完整后状态表示，包含两个互补条件假设——由变化前场景导出的先验假设，以及基于已观测变化后上下文更新的后验假设；用联合嵌入预测损失使两个假设对齐目标潜在表示，从而强化语义一致性、抑制瞬现外观变化。变化由变化前表示、观测到的变化后目标与预测潜在状态之间的不一致性推断，综合变化证据、先验惊奇、上下文更新与预测可靠性四类线索。

Result: 在SYSU-CD、LEVIR-CD+与CDD三个基准上，ChangeJEPA相对结构匹配的孪生解码器基线提升最高达1.51个IoU点，并在三者上均取得最佳IoU与F1。

Conclusion: 实验表明，将变化检测建模为潜在后状态预测优于直接特征比较，验证了预测式联合嵌入范式在抑制瞬现差异、提升变化判别力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection is typically formulated as Siamese feature comparison, where two images are encoded independently and a decoder determines which discrepancies correspond to meaningful change. This is challenging because the encoders preserve both true scene changes and nuisance variations such as illumination, seasonal, radiometric, and geometric differences. We introduce ChangeJEPA, a bitemporal joint-embedding predictive architecture that reformulates change detection as latent post-state prediction. Given a pre-change image and partial post-change evidence, ChangeJEPA predicts the full post-change representation under two complementary conditions: a prior hypothesis derived from the pre-change scene and an updated hypothesis conditioned on observed post-change context. A joint-embedding predictive loss aligns both hypotheses with the target latent representation, encouraging semantic consistency while suppressing transient appearance variations. Change is then inferred from the disagreement among the pre-change representation, the observed post-change target, and the predicted latent states, capturing complementary evidence of change, prior surprise, contextual update, and prediction reliability. Across SYSU-CD, LEVIR-CD+, and CDD, ChangeJEPA consistently outperforms its decoder matched Siamese baseline by up to 1.51 IoU points and achieves the best IoU and F1-score on all three benchmarks, demonstrating the advantage of latent post-state prediction over direct feature comparison. Page: https://buddhi19.github.io/ChangeJEPA

</details>


### [21] [Whose Values Are Generated? Measuring Visual Value Leakage in Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05578)
*Zucan Lyu,Binwei Yao,Rheeya Uppaal,Jing Yao,Xiaoyuan Yi,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多语言基准 VisionValueBench，用 Hofstede 六个文化价值维度衡量 T2I 系统在"价值中立"社会情境提示下生成图像的价值取向，发现普遍存在"视觉价值泄漏"与全球性同质化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 文化表征评测集中于地标、服饰、活动等可识别元素，忽视了生成图像所传达的文化价值；而价值中立提示仍可能隐含特定文化取向，这一盲区缺乏系统度量工具。

Method: 基于 Hofstede 六项文化价值维度（如个人主义—集体主义）构建多语言基准，将中性社会情境操作化为可评估维度；覆盖 960 个社会场景、变化语言与地理线索的提示，并在 6 种 T2I 配置上生成图像进行价值取向分析。

Result: 价值中立提示生成的图像系统性偏向特定取向，六维度一致：平等优于等级（低权力距离）、个体自主优于群体义务（个人主义）、享乐优于约束（放纵）。提示语言影响有限；地理线索会使部分维度向对应文化价值偏移，但整体主导取向基本保持。

Conclusion: T2I 系统在全球范围内传达的文化价值高度同质，存在显著的"视觉价值泄漏"；文化表征评测需从可识别元素扩展到价值层面，多语言/地理提示不足以纠正默认价值偏向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems serve users across languages and cultural contexts. However, evaluations of cultural representation focus mainly on recognizable elements such as landmarks, clothing, and activities, overlooking the cultural values that generated images convey. To bridge this gap, we introduce VisionValueBench, a multilingual benchmark that operationalizes six of Hofstede's cultural value dimensions (e.g., individualism versus collectivism) to investigate how T2I systems frame neutral social situations through a particular cultural orientation. We find that value-neutral prompts, which admit diverse cultural interpretations, nonetheless yield images that favor particular value orientations, a phenomenon we call visual value leakage. We evaluate prompts varying in language and geographic cues across 960 social scenarios and six T2I configurations. Generated images consistently favor the same value orientations across all six dimensions, for example, equality over hierarchy (low power distance), individual autonomy over group obligation (individualism), and enjoyment over restraint (indulgence). Prompt language has limited influence on these orientations, whereas geographic cues shift some dimensions toward the associated cultural values while largely preserving the prevailing orientations. These findings reveal substantial homogeneity in the cultural values conveyed by T2I systems worldwide.

</details>


### [22] [Multimodal Fusion Methods for Whole-Slide Images and Clinical Data in Inflammatory Bowel Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05579)
*Ankana Banerjee,Mark van der Giezen,Ole Gunnar Aasprong,Emiel A. M. Janssen,Øyvind Steinsbø,Tore Grimstad,Kjersti Engan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模块化框架，在相同条件下比较15种多模态融合方法，用于炎症性肠病患者的STRIDE-II缓解预测，融合优于单模态但最佳方法依结局与输入而变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理中多模态融合研究活跃，但融合方法很少在完全相同条件下、跨不同临床终点进行比较，缺乏公平基准。

Method: 模块化框架：以冻结基础模型的patch embedding训练注意力聚合器得到切片级embedding，与每次就诊的临床embedding一同输入15种可互换融合方法（统一输出维度与预测头，决策级方法除外）；在新诊断IBD前瞻性队列中，基于基线或随访数据预测STRIDE-II对齐的缓解结局。

Result: 最佳融合方法macro-F1达0.57–0.75，在6个场景中的4个优于单模态模型，且最佳方法随结局和输入不同而变化；图像依赖度（移除切片embedding后平衡准确率的下降）随缓解类型变化，组织学缓解最高、内镜缓解最低；准确率相近的方法对切片的依赖程度差异很大。

Conclusion: 融合收益具有条件性，不能一概而论；准确率指标无法反映模态依赖差异，需同时评估模态贡献与依赖结构；所提框架为多模态融合的受控比较提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Multimodal AI fusion of histology and clinical data is an active research area in computational pathology, but fusion methods are rarely compared under identical conditions and across different clinical endpoints. We present a modular framework in which slide-level embeddings, obtained by training an attention-based aggregator over patch embeddings from a frozen foundation model, and per-visit clinical embeddings are fed into 15 interchangeable fusion methods with a shared output dimension and prediction head (except decision-level methods). Using a prospective cohort of newly diagnosed inflammatory bowel disease patients, we predicted STRIDE-II-aligned remission outcomes from baseline or follow-up visits. The best fusion method reached macro-F1 of 0.57-0.75, outperforming both single-modality models in four of six scenarios, and differed by outcome and input. Image reliance, the drop in balanced accuracy when the slide embedding is removed, tended to follow the remission type - highest for histological and lowest for endoscopic remission; methods with similar accuracy relied on the slide to very different degrees.

</details>


### [23] [GaussianRenderFormer: Transformer-based Rendering of 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05593)
*Shahaf Valencia Levy,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GaussianRenderFormer，用纯 Transformer 直接从 3D 高斯集合与相机参数生成图像，无需光栅化器；在 Objaverse 上达 38.08 dB，低于光栅化基线 44.02 dB，诊断表明瓶颈在视角相关解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络能否在渲染器中不依赖任何光栅化器的情况下，自行学会渲染 3D 高斯泼溅表示，即以纯学习方式替代传统逐高斯投影与混合的渲染管线。

Method: 每个高斯作为一个 token，输入视角无关（view-independent）的 Transformer 编码器；视角相关的 Transformer 解码器以相机射线的小块（patch）为查询，通过注意力聚合高斯 token 后直接输出像素。训练数据为 Objaverse 的 26,820 个物体，每个裁剪至 20,000 个高斯。另做诊断实验：向解码器显式提供各高斯的投影足迹（footprint）。

Result: 在 300 个未见物体上，相对完整（未裁剪）高斯泼溅的 PSNR 为 38.08 dB；对同一裁剪输入做光栅化的结果为 44.02 dB，差距约 6 dB。该差距并非泛化问题；显式提供高斯投影足迹可提升 1.4 dB，说明瓶颈集中在视角解码器；同时不排除训练不足（under-training）的可能。

Conclusion: 纯 Transformer 渲染 3D 高斯在原理上可行，但尚未达到光栅化的精度；主要瓶颈在于解码器对投影足迹等几何量的隐式推断，显式引入几何提示或更充分训练有望缩小差距。代码、模型与视频已公开。

Abstract: Can a neural network learn to render 3D Gaussian splats on its own, with no rasterizer in the renderer? We present GaussianRenderFormer, a transformer that takes a set of Gaussians and a camera and outputs the image directly. Each Gaussian becomes one token in a view-independent encoder, and a view-dependent decoder turns small patches of camera rays into pixels by attending to those tokens. Trained on 26,820 Objaverse objects pruned to 20,000 Gaussians each, GaussianRenderFormer renders 300 unseen objects at 38.08 dB PSNR against the full, unpruned splats, while rasterizing the same pruned input reaches 44.02 dB. The gap is not one of generalization, and our diagnostics are consistent with a bottleneck in the view decoder: telling it the projected footprint of each Gaussian, which it could in principle infer, adds 1.4 dB. Under-training is not ruled out. Code, trained models and videos: https://svlwoof.github.io/gaussianrenderformer

</details>


### [24] [CAPTOR: text-encoder-free grounding by counting caption co-occurrence over the visual words of a frozen encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05599)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAPTOR 用"视觉词聚类+标题词占比差异"构造词-块打分矩阵，无需文本编码器即可为纯图像编码器提供文本接口，在 COCO/VOC/Cityscapes 及病理切片上领先无训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本-图像接地需要词-块打分矩阵，但该矩阵依赖对齐的文本编码器；许多图像编码器（如仅用切片训练的组织病理模型 UNI）缺乏文本编码器，因而无法做文本接地。

Method: 冻结图像编码器的 patch token 聚类为"视觉词"，用每张带标题图像在各视觉词上的 patch 占比描述图像；某词的签名 = 提到该词的图像与其余图像在该词占比上的标准化差值；矩阵可分解为"词×视觉词"表与查询无关的图像因子，故图像只需编码一次，新增词仅加一行表项。

Result: 在 COCO、Pascal VOC、Cityscapes 上优于所有免训练基线；COCO 单目标单元格上 mIoU 0.565 vs 0.502；三者上均与同骨干的文本对齐训练模型相当（后者需梯度、文本编码器及数倍标题）；在留出乳腺癌切片上比使用病理文本编码器的最强基线高 0.160 mIoU；用 CAPTOR 读出时 UNI 在公共网格上超出此类基线 0.132。

Conclusion: 只要有带标题图像，计数式统计即可为任何无语言训练的编码器赋予文本接口，实现免训练、免文本编码器的文本接地。

Abstract: Grounding a text query in an image needs a matrix scoring every term against every patch. Vision-language models compute it with an aligned text encoder, which many image encoders, such as histopathology-related models trained on slides alone, lack. The Caption-Anchored Patch-Term Occurrence Representation (CAPTOR) estimates the matrix without a text encoder. It clusters a frozen image encoder's patch tokens into visual words and describes each captioned image by its share of patches in each word. A term signature is the standardized difference of those shares between images whose captions mention the term and the rest. The matrix factorizes into a term-by-word table and a query-independent image factor, so an image is encoded once and a new term adds one table row. Against every training-free baseline, CAPTOR leads on Common Objects in Context (COCO), Pascal Visual Object Classes (VOC) and Cityscapes. On COCO, scored on the cells one object owns, it reaches 0.565 mean intersection over union (mIoU) against 0.502. On all three, it is also level with a text alignment trained on the same backbone, which needs gradients, a text encoder and several times the captions. On held-out breast-cancer slides, it exceeds the strongest baseline scoring patches with a pathology text encoder by 0.160 mIoU. Read through CAPTOR, UNI, an encoder trained on slides alone, exceeds such baselines by 0.132 on a common grid. Counting can therefore give a text interface to any image encoder trained without language, wherever captioned images exist.

</details>


### [25] [Spatio-Temporal Multi-Order Interaction for Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05633)
*Kevin D. Nguetche,Lilian Hollard,Eric Granger,Marco Pedersoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoMogaNet 以深度可分离卷积与隐式门控聚合实现通道线性复杂度的多阶时序建模，并提出空时双维交互，在多视频基准上达到或超过SOTA且大幅降低时序分支开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频识别时序方法依赖显式跨通道聚合投影，复杂度随通道数呈二次增长，计算开销大；且CNN偏局部阶交互、Transformer偏全局阶交互，空时表征偏置明显，缺乏均衡的多阶表示。

Method: 以MogaNet的多阶空间表示为起点，提出两点贡献：(1) 时序建模采用并行的多阶处理，仅用depthwise卷积配合隐式门控（implicit gating）聚合替代显式跨通道投影，复杂度对通道数呈线性；(2) 设计空间与时间维度之间的对偶交互（dual interaction），在不增加计算成本前提下改善空时表征。

Result: 在UCF101、HMDB51、Kinetics-400、Diving48多个视频基准上，性能与当前最优动作识别方法持平或更优，同时显著降低时序分支的计算开销。

Conclusion: 以线性复杂度的多阶时序建模与空时对偶交互可在不牺牲精度的前提下高效提升视频识别性能，为轻量高效视频骨干设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Many temporal approaches for video recognition rely on explicit cross-channel aggregation projections, which have quadratic complexity in the number of channels, introducing significant computational overhead. In addition, the spatio-temporal representation used for video recognition is biased either toward local order interactions (for CNNs) or toward global order interactions (for transformers). Starting from the multi-order spatial representation of MogaNet, our method introduces two complementary contributions to tackle these problems. First, for the temporal modelling, we introduce parallel multi-order processing with depthwise-only convolutions and implicit gating-based aggregation, which is linear in the number of channels, inducing a considerable computational reduction. Second, we propose a dual interaction between spatial and temporal dimensions, which allows a better spatio-temporal representation without increasing the computational cost. By combining these two contributions, our proposed VideoMogaNet achieves performance on par with or better than state-of-the-art action recognition approaches across multiple video benchmarks (UCF101, HMDB51, Kinetics-400, Diving48), while considerably reducing the cost of the temporal branch.

</details>


### [26] [ARIDA - Active railway infrastructure dataset annotation using neural network predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05646)
*Maximilian Dornik,Christian Sieberichs,Simon Geerkens,Alexander Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向铁路基础设施的半自动标注框架ARIDA，将实例分割网络的预测转换为包围盒、多边形、折线及成对折线，并通过置信度阈值与轮廓一致性质量分数作为准入条件，结合主动学习的不确定性排序与新颖的数量控制机制，在无真值条件下自动接受简单样本、人工聚焦困难样本，从而大幅降低标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路感知需要大规模综合图像数据集，但人工标注（尤其是轨道折线等复杂基础设施几何）耗时严重，成为数据集构建瓶颈。

Method: 构建铁路专用的半自动标注流水线：以mask scoring实例分割网络的实例预测为基础，生成多种几何标注形式（包围盒、多边形、折线、成对折线）；采用置信度阈值与独特的轮廓一致性质量分数作为验收门控，无需真值即可保证自动标注可靠性；引入主动学习循环按不确定性对剩余图像排序，并通过新颖的数量控制机制自适应调整每轮人工查询数量，实现简单图像自动接受、人工集中于困难样本。

Result: 在不重叠、多样化图像与时间序列、冗余图像两类场景上实验，量化了工作量减少与精度权衡：不重叠场景约减少三分之二标注量，序列场景最高减少93%；并给出初始标注预算与停止准则的指导，以避免过度标注。

Conclusion: 该框架可在无真值条件下有效降低铁路基础设施数据集标注成本，并在不同数据分布下保持可控的精度权衡，为标注预算与终止策略提供实践指导。

Abstract: Modern railway perception requires large comprehensive image datasets, but creating them is slowed by manual labeling, especially for track polylines and other complex infrastructure geometry. This paper presents the Active Railway Infrastructure Dataset Annotation framework, a railway-specific semi-automated pipeline that converts instance predictions from a mask scoring instance segmentation network into bounding-boxes, polygons, polylines, and paired polylines. To keep automatic track geometry annotation reliable without ground truth, it applies confidence thresholds and an unique contour-agreement quality score as an acceptance gate. An active learning loop ranks remaining images by uncertainty and adapts the number of manual queries per cycle. This is achieved through a novel quantity control mechanism, in which easy images are auto-accepted and human effort targets hard cases. Experiments on disjoint, diverse imagery and time-sequential, redundant imagery quantify effort reduction and accuracy trade-offs, achieving about two thirds reduction in the disjoint regime and up to 93% in the sequential regime. The study also provides guidance for initial label budgets and stopping to avoid over-annotation.

</details>


### [27] [NitroFusion2: Bringing Adversarial Loss to the Text Stream of Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05648)
*Dar-Yen Chen,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本流对抗蒸馏（TSAD）与面向方面提示判别（ACD），在 NitroFusion 专家判别器基础上构建 NitroFusion2，将少步蒸馏从 4-8 步降至 1-2 步，一步即可生成清晰文字与丰富细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗扩散蒸馏通常依赖图像特征做判别。作者注意到多模态扩散 Transformer（MMDiT）的文本流通过联合注意力与图像耦合，因而可能提供新的、可控的对抗表示，以提高蒸馏步数压缩的极限。

Method: 1) TSAD：以中间文本 token 状态作为对抗判别表示；由于文本流已"读取"图像且仍受文本条件约束，改变条件会改变其从图像中提取的信息。2) ACD：让判别器以样本的不同方面（排版、物体、风格等）为条件，引导对抗训练强化的属性。3) 基于 NitroFusion 的专家判别器设计，将二者整合为 NitroFusion2。

Result: NitroFusion2 在质量相当的情况下把少步蒸馏由 4-8 步降到 1-2 步；单步生成可得到锐利的渲染文字与丰富的细粒度细节，使一步生成具备实用性；两步生成在各评测基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: MMDiT 的文本流是一种强有力且可引导的对抗扩散蒸馏表示，TSAD 与 ACD 为极端步数压缩提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Adversarial diffusion distillation has conventionally relied on image features for discrimination. We revisit this choice for multimodal diffusion transformers (MMDiTs), whose text stream becomes image-dependent through joint attention. We introduce Text-Stream Adversarial Distillation (TSAD), which exploits intermediate text-token states as a new adversarial representation. Because the text stream has already read the image while remaining tied to the text condition, changing that condition changes what information it extracts from the image. Building on this property, we introduce Aspect-Cued Discrimination (ACD), which conditions the discriminator on different aspects of the sample, such as typography, objects, or style, to steer which properties are emphasized during adversarial training. Building on the expert-discriminator design of NitroFusion, we integrate TSAD and ACD in NitroFusion2. NitroFusion2 brings few-step distillation from the conventional 4-8-step regime down to just 1-2 steps at comparable quality. At one step, it produces sharp rendered text and rich fine-grained details, making one-step generation practical rather than merely a stress test for distillation. At two steps, it remains competitive across the evaluated benchmarks. These results establish the MMDiT text stream as a powerful and steerable representation for adversarial diffusion distillation.

</details>


### [28] [Toward an Optimal Configuration for Tree Species Classification from Bark Images: A Leakage-Safe Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05680)
*Maeve Côté*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在BarkNet 1.0（20种）上用ConvNeXt-V2骨干做系统消融，考察patch尺寸、聚合策略、训练阶段、骨干容量、整图基线和推理patch数，得出"尽可能大的patch（约512px）+nano骨干"的推荐配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树皮照片的树种自动识别对林业清点与木材加工有实用价值，但深度学习方法中影响精度的设计选择缺乏系统研究；且部分既有结果可能因同一树干图像在训练/测试间泄漏而被高估，需要一套泄漏安全、可复现的基准参照。

Method: 在BarkNet 1.0（20类）上采用树级、泄漏安全划分方案，以ConvNeXt-V2为骨干，通过五折交叉验证逐项消融：patch尺寸、逐patch预测的图级聚合策略、单阶段与两阶段训练、骨干容量（pico至tiny）、整图基线、推理时可用patch数量。

Result: 逐patch精度随patch尺寸单调上升（160px为75.6%→512px为89.6%），图级精度同步上升并在512px附近于约94.9%趋于饱和；学习式聚合较多数投票最多提升0.7个百分点，且随patch增大、注意力趋于均匀而优势缩小；骨干容量（pico至tiny）影响不足1个百分点，两阶段训练相对单阶段无优势；整图分类（91.9%）劣于patch聚合（缺少可平均的bag）；精度随推理patch数增加而提升，约在64个时饱和。

Conclusion: 给出简洁的推荐配置：patch尽可能大且仍保留足够数量用于平均（此处约512px），配合nano骨干；同时量化了学习式聚合与模型容量的二阶作用，为实践者和后续研究提供可复现的参照点。

Abstract: Background: The automatic identification of tree species from bark photographs is of practical interest for forestry inventory and the wood-processing industry, yet the design choices that govern the accuracy of deep-learning approaches have not been studied systematically, and some prior results may be inflated by leakage of images of the same trunk across training and test partitions. Methods: A systematic ablation study is conducted on the public BarkNet 1.0 dataset (20 species) under the established tree-level, leakage-safe partitioning scheme. Using ConvNeXt-V2 backbones, the following factors are varied in turn and evaluated by five-fold cross-validation: patch size, the strategy by which per-patch predictions are aggregated into an image-level decision, the training regime (single- versus two-stage), backbone capacity, the whole-image baseline, and the number of patches available at inference. Results: Per-patch accuracy rises monotonically with patch size (from 75.6% at 160 px to 89.6% at 512 px), and image-level accuracy rises in step, plateauing near 94.9% around 512 px. Learned aggregation exceeds majority voting by at most 0.7 pp, an advantage that shrinks as patches grow and attention becomes near-uniform. Backbone capacity (pico to tiny) alters accuracy by less than a point, and two-stage training offers no advantage over one-stage. Whole-image classification (91.9%) trails patch-based aggregation, since it admits no bag to average over; accuracy increases with the number of patches at inference, saturating around 64. Conclusions: The study yields a simple recommended configuration: patches as large as possible while leaving enough to average over (around 512 px here), with a nano backbone. It also quantifies the second-order role of learned aggregation and model capacity, providing a reproducible reference point for practitioners and future research

</details>


### [29] [TRUST-Stereo: Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05689)
*Pouya Sohrabipour,Yihong Feng,Chaitanya Kumar Reddy Pallerla,Tuan Dang,Ngan Le,Dongyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRUST-Stereo通过不确定性引导的残差搜索模块（UGRSM）修正粗到细立体匹配中传播视差的误差，并结合Senior-Junior教师助教知识蒸馏把大模型单目表征迁移到轻量骨干，仅用1.4M合成数据即在多个基准取得高效方法中的领先精度（约50ms/对）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细（coarse-to-fine）立体匹配效率高，但对传播视差的误差敏感：一旦上层视差估计有偏，真实对应点可能落在后续局部搜索窗口之外，导致不可恢复的匹配失败。同时，高效模型往往依赖大规模数据（含真实伪标签）才能获得好泛化。

Method: 1) Uncertainty-Guided Residual Search Module（UGRSM）：估计传播视差的可靠性（不确定性），并预测残差偏移以自适应地重新定位局部代价体中心，从而把真实对应拉回搜索范围；2) 两阶段Senior-Junior教师助教知识蒸馏：将大型基础模型的丰富单目表征迁移到轻量骨干网络，实现快速立体匹配。

Result: 在KITTI 2012、KITTI 2015、ETH3D、Middlebury上表现出强零样本泛化；仅用1.4M合成立体图像对训练，在多项指标上达到SOTA并优于使用更大数据集（含真实伪标签）的高效方法：KITTI 2015 EPE 0.93，ETH3D Bad 1.0 为 2.13%，Middlebury Bad 2.0 为 6.79%，推理约50 ms/对。

Conclusion: 以不确定性引导的残差搜索缓解粗到细视差传播误差，配合助教式蒸馏注入单目先验，可在极低数据成本下实现高精度、实时（~50ms）且泛化良好的立体匹配，为高效立体匹配提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine stereo matching enables efficient disparity estimation but remains sensitive to errors in propagated disparity, which may place ground-truth correspondences outside subsequent local search ranges. We propose TRUST-Stereo (Teacher-Assisted Robust Uncertainty-Guided Search and Transfer for Fast Stereo Matching), which addresses this limitation through an Uncertainty-Guided Residual Search Module (UGRSM). UGRSM estimates the reliability of propagated disparity and predicts residual offsets to adaptively relocate local cost-volume centers. Additionally, we introduce a two-stage Senior-Junior Teacher Assistant knowledge distillation strategy to transfer rich monocular representations from a large foundation model to a lightweight backbone to achieve fast stereo matching performance. Experiments on KITTI 2012, KITTI 2015, ETH3D, and Middlebury demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization. Using only 1.4M synthetic stereo pairs, TRUST-Stereo achieves state-of-the-art results on several benchmark metrics, outperforming competing efficient methods trained with substantially larger datasets, including real-world pseudo-labeled samples. Specifically, it achieves a KITTI 2015 EPE of 0.93, ETH3D Bad 1.0 of 2.13% and Middlebury Bad 2.0 of 6.79% while maintaining inference at approximately 50 ms per stereo pair.

</details>


### [30] [Anchored Slice Propagation with Boundary Transport for 3D CT Segmentation from Three Orthogonal Annotated Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05693)
*Tushar Kataria,Md Rakibul Haque,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以固定预训练亲和力骨干为基座的Sli2Vol改进：用正交标注锚定各传播链并据此加权融合，同时比较SDF与可学习轮廓两种边界几何传输方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐片亲和力传播中，每步预测成为下一步输入，误差会沿链累积；正交标注不仅提供三个起点掩膜，其与链的交点处已含正确标签，可用于纠偏与融合置信度；此外二值掩膜丢失到边界距离信息，难以跟踪切片间渐变形状。

Method: 固定预训练亲和力骨干。锚定：在链穿越其他已标注平面处校正预测，并用其与这些标签的一致性加权融合。边界几何传输两法对比：(1)截断符号距离场(SDF)经稠密亲和力平均传播；(2)顶点依同一亲和力运动线索的轮廓跟踪，从手写规则出发，利用506例CT体数据中的超体素伪目标学习运动校正，无需人工器官标签。

Result: 在约403例CT扫描、17个腹部结构、2,091个留出结构实例上：锚定将平均Dice从正交掩膜传播的0.737提升至0.769，且每个结构均有改善；学习轮廓跟踪使食管Dice提升5.7%、下腔静脉提升5.9%，SDF传输使右肾上腺提升2.8%；三种锚定方法平均Dice相近(0.769–0.772)，但轮廓跟踪在肾脏、胃和肝血管树上表现较差。

Conclusion: 利用交点标签锚定并加权融合可稳定提升各向传播精度；边界几何传输的收益具结构特异性，轮廓跟踪利于细长/小器官，SDF利于形状简单器官，在复杂结构上不及掩膜锚定。

Abstract: Sli2Vol propagates a binary mask through affinities between adjacent slices, but an error can persist because each prediction becomes the input to the next step. Three orthogonal annotations reduce dependence on any one chain, yet they offer more than three starting masks: where a chain crosses another annotated plane, the correct label is already known. Those labels could correct drift during propagation and indicate how much to trust each chain at fusion. Between annotated planes, the information carried by the chain may matter as well. A binary mask records foreground membership but discards distance to the boundary, a cue that could help follow gradual shape changes between slices.We address these two sources of error with a fixed pretrained affinity backbone. We anchor each chain by correcting its prediction where it crosses another annotated plane, then use its agreement with those labels to weight fusion. We compare this anchored mask with two ways of transporting boundary geometry: a truncated signed-distance field (SDF) propagated by dense affinity averaging, and a contour whose vertices follow motion cues from the same affinity. The contour tracker starts from a hand-crafted rule and learns a motion correction from supervoxel pseudo-objects in 506 CT volumes, without manual organ labels. On 2,091 held-out structure instances from approximately 403 CT scans spanning 17 abdominal structures, anchoring raises mean Dice from 0.737 with orthogonal mask propagation to 0.769 and improves every structure. Beyond the anchored mask, learned contour tracking improves Dice by 5.7\% for the esophagus and 5.9\% for the postcava, while SDF transport improves right-adrenal Dice by 2.8\%. The anchored methods have similar mean Dice (0.769--0.772); contour tracking performs worse on the kidneys, stomach, and hepatic vessel tree.

</details>


### [31] [GeoDIVE: Geospatial Descent Informed by Value Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05697)
*Hamza Iqbal,Dan Cher,Eric Xing,Pranavi Kolouju,Abby Stylianou,Robert Pless,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoDIVE 将多分辨率卫星影像库视为森林结构，用最佳优先遍历决定编码哪些瓦片，并借助轻量模型预测子树内最优查询相似度的乐观上界，从而在低成本下实现多尺度地理空间检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业、基础设施规划、环境监测与灾害响应等任务需要在大范围内寻找特定目标、结构与空间模式，但这些特征分散、尺度差异巨大，朴素地嵌入、维护与检索代价高昂。

Method: 把多分辨率卫星影像库建模为树/森林，采用最佳优先（best-first）遍历选择待编码瓦片；在每个访问节点上，用查询表示与子树表示训练轻量模型，预测该子树后代中可能达到的最佳查询相似度的乐观估计，从而优先扩展有潜力的子树。

Result: 在图像查询下，GeoDIVE 以约10%的编码成本达到穷举搜索效果；在图像与开放词汇文本两种模态下，各预算档位均优于所有对比基线。同时发布 Git-10M 的物体级图像与文本查询集（基于 Microsoft 建筑轮廓与 OpenStreetMap 多边形，配 VLM 生成描述）。

Conclusion: GeoDIVE 表明基于乐观相似度预测的层级最佳优先检索可显著降低多尺度地理空间搜索成本，配套基准与代码模型将公开，支持后续评估。

Abstract: Finding specific objects, structures, and spatial patterns supports critical work in agriculture, infrastructure planning, environmental monitoring, and disaster response. These features, however, are often dispersed across very large regions and span dramatically different spatial scales, which defines a search space that is costly to embed, maintain, and search naively. We introduce GeoDIVE, a model that treats a multiresolution satellite image gallery as a forest and uses best-first traversal to determine which tiles to encode. At each visited node, a lightweight model then predicts an optimistic estimate of the best query similarity hidden among its descendants using the query and subtree representations. In evaluations with image and open-vocabulary text queries, GeoDIVE matches exhaustive search at 10% of the encoding cost for image queries and leads all tested baselines at every budget in both modalities. To support future evaluation of multiscale geospatial search, we also release object-level image and text queries for Git-10M, constructed from Microsoft building footprints and OpenStreetMap polygons with VLM-generated captions. Source code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [32] [Mitigating Spurious Invariance in Contrastive Learning: A Probabilistic Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05734)
*Wei Wei,Yading Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像对比自监督学习中增强可能抑制细微关键特征导致的伪不变性问题，将特征嵌入建模为概率分布而非固定向量，并提出统一概率框架整合多种SSL方法，在分割与分类任务上优于现有SSL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像人工标注成本高昂，自监督学习受到关注；对比学习依赖不同增强视图间的不变性，但医学图像中细微而关键的特征可能在增强过程中被抑制，使这种不变性成为伪不变性。

Method: 将学习到的特征嵌入编码为概率分布而非固定向量，以捕获更丰富、更细致的信息；并提出一个统一的概率框架，将多种自监督学习方法无缝整合。

Result: 在多个数据集上的医学图像分割与分类等下游任务中优于当前最先进的SSL方法；并通过更强增强下的鲁棒性、有限微调标签下的标签效率以及计算开销权衡等额外分析进行验证。

Conclusion: 概率分布式特征表示可缓解对比学习中由增强引起的关键医学特征抑制问题，统一概率框架为多种SSL方法提供了有效整合途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Owing to the prohibitive cost of manual annotation for enormous medical images, self-supervised learning (SSL) has gained substantial attention and shown promise in various medical imaging tasks. Among SSL approaches, contrastive learning has emerged as a prominent one, encouraging models to encode semantic information that remains invariant between different augmented views. However, this invariance can be spurious for medical images, as subtle but critical medical features can be suppressed during augmentations. In this paper, we mitigate this issue by encoding the learned feature embeddings as probability distributions rather than fixed vectors, thus capturing a richer and more nuanced spectrum of information. Furthermore, we propose a unified probabilistic framework that seamlessly integrates multiple SSL approaches. Our method achieves superior performance over the state-of-the-art SSL methods on various downstream tasks across multiple datasets, including medical image segmentation and classification. We further validate the method through additional analyses of robustness to stronger augmentations, label efficiency under limited fine-tuning labels, and computational trade-offs. The code is released at https://github.com/wwzjer/ProbSSL.

</details>


### [33] [C-WFDM: A ControlNet-Guided Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model for Multimodal Brain MRI Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05737)
*Muhammad Nabi Yasinzai,Remika Mito,Mangor Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 C-WFDM（基于 WFDM 的 ControlNet 扩展），用于受试者特异性的缺失模态补全，在并行数据集可用性模式下生成 T2w、FLAIR 与 T1Gd，并在 BrainScape 实验中取得最优 T2w/FLAIR 合成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开与汇集的神经影像数据集中各 MRI 模态可获得性不均衡（T1w 普遍、T2w/FLAIR 较少、T1Gd 稀缺），参与者具有不同的模态组合。现有缺失模态基准多从完整四模态病例中人为扣留模态来模拟缺失，无法反映汇集数据集中真实的可用性模式，存在评估与建模的偏差。

Method: 以 WFDM（小波融合扩散模型）为基础，构建 ControlNet 扩展 C-WFDM。预训练 WFDM 主干保留目标模态与元数据条件；目标模态专用的 ControlNet 适配器以同一受试者的其他 MRI 对比作为条件引导合成。合成流程为：T1w→T2w，T1w+T2w→FLAIR，T1w+T2w+FLAIR→T1Gd；优先使用真实采集的条件模态，仅在必要时使用已生成的中间模态。基于 BrainScape 快照（157 个公开数据集、45,880 例预处理扫描）训练。与 ALDM、cWDM、Pix2Pix 对比，指标为配准后的 SSIM、PSNR、MAE。

Result: 在 BrainScape 补全实验中，C-WFDM 在评测模型中取得最优的 T2w 与 FLAIR 结果，SSIM 分别为 0.9232 与 0.8904；对比方法为 ALDM、cWDM、Pix2Pix，评价指标为配对 SSIM、PSNR 与 MAE。

Conclusion: 针对真实汇集数据集中非完备模态组合的补全任务，目标特定的 ControlNet 适配器结合预训练扩散主干可在保持元数据条件的同时有效利用同受试者跨模态信息，T2w 与 FLAIR 合成性能优于现有方法；T1Gd 合成亦纳入框架但摘要未报告其量化结果。

Abstract: Multimodal brain MRI provides complementary anatomical, tissue, and pathological information to support downstream artificial intelligence (AI) applications. However, modality availability is uneven across public and pooled neuroimaging datasets: T1-weighted (T1w) MRI is widely represented, T2-weighted (T2w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) MRI are less consistently available, and gadolinium-enhanced T1-weighted (T1Gd) MRI is relatively scarce. Pooled datasets therefore contain participants with different combinations of acquired MRI contrasts, reflecting variation in the study designs, acquisition protocols, and clinical or research objectives of the contributing studies. Many missing-modality benchmarks simulate incomplete MRI by withholding contrasts from complete four-modality cases, which may not represent the observed availability patterns in pooled datasets. To address this gap, we introduce C-WFDM, a ControlNet extension of our Wavelet-Fusion Diffusion Model (WFDM), for subject-specific missing-modality completion. C-WFDM is built on an earlier BrainScape snapshot comprising 157 public MRI datasets and 45,880 preprocessed scans. The pretrained WFDM backbone retains target-modality and metadata conditioning, while target-specific ControlNet adapters use MRI contrasts from the same participant to guide synthesis. C-WFDM synthesizes T2w from T1w, FLAIR from T1w and T2w, and T1Gd from T1w, T2w, and FLAIR. Acquired conditioning contrasts are used when available, and generated intermediate contrasts are used only when required. We compare C-WFDM with ALDM, cWDM, and Pix2Pix using paired structural similarity index (SSIM), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), and mean absolute error (MAE). In the BrainScape completion experiment, C-WFDM achieves the best T2w and FLAIR results among the evaluated models, with SSIM values of 0.9232 and 0.8904, respectively.

</details>


### [34] [Learning Hierarchical Patch Splitting Policies for Faster Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05742)
*Aditya Gupta,Jean Sebastien Dandurand,Kai Qiu,Rohan Choudhury,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPLIT 提出学习式图像 tokenization 策略，依据模型判断的任务相关信息（而非像素统计）分配 token 分辨率，在分类任务上以少 30% 的 token 达到全分辨率 ViT 精度，并在 COCO、ADE20K 上验证泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 的计算瓶颈在于输入 token 序列长度，标准 patchification 用统一 patch 尺寸处理全图，忽略区域内容差异；已有自适应 patch 方法虽能减少 token，但依赖局部熵等噪声大的像素级启发式决策，与任务相关性脱节。

Method: 提出 Split Policy Learned Image Tokenization（SPLIT），学习一个策略，以模型对任务相关信息的理解为依据，决定各区域 patch 的粗细（token 分辨率分配），从而生成更短且与任务相关内容更匹配的 token 序列。

Result: 在图像分类上，SPLIT 在减少 30% token 的同时匹配全分辨率 ViT 基线精度；在 COCO 与 ADE20K 稠密预测任务上取得有竞争力精度。

Conclusion: 语义对齐的 patchification 决策优于像素级启发式，且能良好泛化到分类之外的任务，验证了以模型语义理解为依据进行计算分配的可行性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are bottlenecked by the length of their input token sequence. Standard patchification fixes this length with a single patch size applied across the image, regardless of regional content. Prior work on adaptive patch sizing reduces token count by assigning coarser patches to visually simple regions, but relies on noisy pixel-level heuristics such as local entropy to make patchification decisions. We argue that this compute allocation should be grounded in the model's interpretation of task relevant information, not in raw pixel statistics. We introduce Split Policy Learned Image Tokenization which learns to allocate token resolution based on what the model finds meaningful, producing shorter token sequences that are better matched to task-relevant content. SPLIT matches the accuracy of full-resolution ViT classification baselines while using 30% fewer tokens. The same gains hold on dense prediction: SPLIT achieves competitive accuracy on COCO and ADE20K, demonstrating that semantically aligned patchification generalises well beyond classification.

</details>


### [35] [Decompose the Distillation: Interpretable Single-Pass Guidance for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05755)
*Wuyang Zhang,Xiaolin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CtrlFlow 提出一种单次前向的引导方法，保留 CFG 可分离的基础流与引导方向接口，通过共享冻结主干上的两个轻量 LoRA 适配器同时预测二者，并配合多尺度蒸馏损失、尺度自适应调制与时间网格扭曲实现任意尺度可调的低成本采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
