Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikePose：帧重置单步脉冲编码器 + 运动对齐热图记忆(MAM)，以91.4%算术能耗下降换取仅1.12点PCK_HB(0.5)损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频人体姿态估计需在定位精度、运动质量与能耗之间取得平衡；脉冲神经网络(SNN)在大规模、非事件驱动视频任务上与传统ANN仍存性能差距，需要轻量化设计弥合该差距。

Method: 采用帧重置(frame-reset)、单步(single-step)脉冲编码器提供逐帧空间证据；时间推理被移出脉冲骨干，交由紧凑轻量的运动对齐热图记忆(MAM)模块完成——MAM估计关节位移、对齐历史热图并与当前预测自适应融合。

Result: 在MPII与固定NTU60 Cross-Subject子集上评估空间精度、运动误差与估计算术能耗：相对同架构带MAM的ANN，SpikePose降低约91.4%算术能耗，PCK_HB(0.5)仅下降1.12点；在SpikePose内部，加入MAM使速度误差与加速度误差分别降低28.13%与31.46%，定位精度略有提升，代价仅为0.003M参数与每帧0.0023分析性GFLOPs。

Conclusion: 将时间建模外置于脉冲骨干的轻量MAM设计，可在保持接近ANN精度的同时显著降低能耗，为SNN在非事件驱动视频姿态估计中的实用化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Efficient video human pose estimation must balance localisation accuracy, motion quality, and energy cost. We present SpikePose, a lightweight video pose estimator that pairs a frame-reset, single-step spiking encoder with motion-aligned heatmap memory (MAM). The encoder supplies per-frame spatial evidence, while MAM estimates joint displacement, aligns historical heatmaps, and adaptively fuses them with current predictions. By confining temporal reasoning to a compact, lightweight module outside the spiking backbone, SpikePose helps bridge the gap between spiking and classical Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) on large-scale, non-event-driven video tasks. We evaluate spatial accuracy, motion errors, and estimated arithmetic energy on MPII and a fixed NTU60 Cross-Subject subset. Against the architecture-matched ANN with MAM, SpikePose cuts estimated arithmetic energy by 91.4% at a cost of just 1.12 points in head-bone-normalised PCK at 0.5 threshold (PCK_HB(0.5)). Within SpikePose, adding MAM reduces velocity and acceleration errors by 28.13% and 31.46% while slightly improving localisation, at a cost of only 0.003M parameters and 0.0023 analytical GFLOPs per frame. Code will be released on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [103] [Learning to Track Cells from Sparse Annotations in 3D Time-Lapse Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06624)
*Yusuke Uchida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个细胞追踪流水线：融合2.5D/3D检测（抑制未标注细胞处的错误背景监督）、基于稠密光流/匹配模型/分裂前后特征的概率校准，以及以评价指标为代理目标的连接与分裂联合选择，在斑马鱼胚胎数据5折交叉验证达0.9762。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从3D延时显微图像重建细胞谱系需区分细胞运动与分裂并推断时序一致的连接；但仅有部分细胞被标注时，将未标注区域当作背景或错误对应来训练会引入有害监督信号。

Method: 1) 检测：结合2.5D与3D模型输出，训练时抑制未标注细胞处的错误背景监督；2) 校准：用稠密光流运动特征、学习型细胞匹配模型的对应特征、以及分裂前细胞/分裂母细胞/分裂后子细胞识别模型特征，校准普通连接与分裂概率；3) 联合选择：在由评价指标导出的代理目标下，联合选择普通连接、以子细胞对表示的分裂及节点纳入，之后进行间隙填补与时间坐标细化。

Result: 199个视频（2个斑马鱼胚胎）5折交叉验证总体得分0.9762；在候选与校准值固定时，联合选择比允许替换已有分裂并重优化普通连接的顺序方法高0.0105，提升主要来自分裂的找回。

Conclusion: 稀疏标注下以抑制错误背景监督的检测、特征驱动概率校准与基于评价指标的联合选择相结合，可有效重建细胞谱系；联合优化分裂与连接优于顺序式处理。

Abstract: Reconstructing cell lineages from 3D time-lapse microscopy requires distinguishing cell motion from division and inferring temporally consistent connections. When only a subset of cells is annotated, however, training procedures that treat unannotated regions as background or incorrect correspondences can be problematic. We propose a cell tracking pipeline that integrates detection from sparse annotations, calibration of candidate connections using image features, and joint selection of lineage graph events. For detection, we combine the outputs of 2.5D and 3D models trained to suppress erroneous background supervision at unannotated cells. We then calibrate ordinary link and division probabilities using motion features from dense flow, correspondence features from a learned cell-matching model, and features from models that identify cells immediately before division, dividing parents, and daughters immediately after division. Ordinary links, divisions represented as daughter pairs, and node inclusion are selected jointly under a surrogate objective derived from the evaluation metric, followed by gap filling and temporal coordinate refinement. The pipeline achieves an overall score of 0.9762 in 5-fold cross-validation on 199 videos from 2 zebrafish embryos. With candidates and calibrated values held fixed, joint selection outperforms a sequential method that allows replacement of existing divisions and reoptimization of ordinary links by 0.0105. The improvement arises primarily from division recovery.

</details>


### [104] [Trick-or-Treat: Adversarial Evaluation of Multimodal Medical Reasoning with Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06631)
*Oishi Banerjee,Eujin Lee,Sung Eun Kim,Alexandra N. Willauer,Christine Y. Zhou,Zhizhou Yang,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Trick-or-Treat对抗性多模态医学基准，通过合成临床照片与真实胸片配对，揭示前沿模型在跨模态推理中的幻觉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态医学数据集很少配对不同图像模态（如胸片与外部临床照片），导致难以评估跨模态推理能力，也无法暴露模型的幻觉问题。

Method: 构建Trick-or-Treat基准：针对多种疾病，各构造两组图像对——共享同一张真实胸片、但配以不同的合成临床照片。匹配对中两张图均指向同一诊断；不匹配对中照片呈现无法归入统一诊断的替代性表现。利用合成生成保持人物特征与拍摄场景一致，以减少混杂变量。

Result: 在三个前沿模型上，不匹配对中模型对临床显著照片表现的幻觉率为45–57.5%，显著高于同一照片单独呈现时的25–37.5%；面对不匹配对时模型倾向忽略矛盾，并幻觉出更契合胸片的发现（如将痤疮误读为结痂性皮损以支持水痘诊断）。

Conclusion: Trick-or-Treat既揭示了前沿模型医学推理中令人担忧的错误模式，也验证了合成图像用于对抗性多模态评估的可行性。

Abstract: We used medical image synthesis to create a challenging multimodal evaluation framework that exposes hallucination behaviors in frontier models. Evaluating multimodal reasoning has historically been difficult because existing medical datasets rarely pair different image modalities, such as radiographs and external clinical photographs. To address this gap, we introduce Trick-or-Treat, an adversarial multimodal medical benchmark that pairs real chest radiographs with synthetic clinical photographs to challenge models' cross-modal reasoning. For each of a wide range of conditions, we created two pairs of images, where the pairs share a single chest radiograph but contain different external photographs. In matched pairs, the chest radiograph and photograph both contain findings suggesting the same disease (e.g., varicella pneumonia suggested by lung opacities and crusted skin lesions seen in chickenpox). In mismatched pairs, the photograph depicts an alternative clinical finding that would not lead to the same unifying diagnosis (e.g. replacing crusted skin lesions with ordinary acne). Synthetic photograph generation allows clinical findings to be varied while the person's features and photographic setting stay consistent, reducing confounding. In an investigation of three frontier models, we found that models hallucinated clinically significant photograph findings in 45-57.5% of mismatched pairs, up from 25-37.5% for the same photographs shown alone. When faced with mismatched pairs, models tended to ignore mismatches and instead hallucinated findings that better matched the radiograph (e.g. misreading acne as crusted skin lesions to justify diagnosing chickenpox). Trick-or-Treat both demonstrates concerning error pattern in frontier models' medical reasoning and provides a proof of concept for the use of synthetic images in adversarial multimodal evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yuting Wan,Liguo Sun,Zao Zhang,Yueyang Wu,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UCP框架，以观测不确定性与判别不确定性刻画区域协作价值，生成请求/供给图实现高效通信，并用U-Fusion模块融合，在3D检测与轨迹预测上取得更优性能-带宽权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机协同感知受带宽与算力严格约束，通信效率是关键瓶颈。现有空间选择方法主要聚焦前景区域以减少漏检，忽视了感知模糊区域，导致误检问题未被解决。

Method: 构建不确定性驱动的协作价值度量：观测不确定性用于识别传感器受限区域，判别不确定性用于刻画决策边界附近的模型歧义；据此生成空间请求图与供给图以指导智能体间通信；接收到的消息通过不确定性引导的交叉注意力模块U-Fusion进行聚合。

Result: 在3D目标检测与轨迹预测任务的三个基准上实验，展现出更优的性能-带宽权衡，并具备较高可解释性与较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将通信选择从单纯前景导向扩展为不确定性导向，可同时缓解漏检与误检，为资源受限下的无人机协同感知提供高效、可解释的通用方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based collaborative perception enables multiple agents to share complementary perceptual information, overcoming limitations such as occlusion and limited sensing range. Communication efficiency is a critical bottleneck, as UAVs operate under stringent bandwidth and computational constraints. However, existing spatial selection methods focus predominantly on foreground regions to reduce missed detections, neglecting perceptually ambiguous regions and leaving false detections unresolved. We propose UCP, an uncertainty-driven framework that characterizes collaborative value through observational uncertainty, identifying sensor-limited regions, and discriminative uncertainty, capturing model-level ambiguity near the decision boundary. These estimates drive spatial request and supply maps for efficient inter-agent communication, and the received messages are aggregated via U-Fusion, an uncertainty-guided cross-attention module. Experiments on three benchmarks across 3D object detection and trajectory prediction demonstrate a superior performance-bandwidth trade-off with high interpretability and strong generalizability.

</details>


### [106] [Human Motion Prediction via Continual Prior Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06637)
*Jianwei Tang,Jian-Fang Hu,Tianming Liang,Xiaotong Lin,Jiangxin Sun,Wei-Shi Zheng,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Continual Prior Compensation (CPC) 及升级版 CPC++ 时序持续学习框架，将人体运动预测按时间跨度拆分为子任务分阶段训练，并用可学习的 Prior Compensation Factor 显式补偿先验遗忘，在三个基准数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法把不同时间步的预测当作单一任务同时学习，导致远期（long-term）预测的学习干扰近未来（short-term）预测的学习，限制短期预测性能。

Method: 将不同时间跨度的预测划分为若干子任务，采用多阶段渐进训练；引入可学习随机变量 Prior Compensation Factor (PCF) 显式度量先验知识损失，并从理论上证明其可通过最小化目标函数的合理上界与模型参数联合优化；进一步提出 CPC++，用 Fine-Grained PCF (FGPCF) 对每个子任务的先验信息损失分别估计。

Result: 在三个 HMP 基准数据集上，结合 PGBIG、siMLPe、MotionMixer、LTD 等多个 SOTA 主干模型的实验表明，框架具有有效性与灵活性。

Conclusion: CPC/CPC++ 可灵活集成于不同 HMP 主干模型并适配多种数据集与应用，缓解渐进训练中的先验遗忘问题。

Abstract: Human Motion Prediction (HMP) aims to predict future human poses at different moments according to observed past motion sequences. Previous approaches mainly treated the prediction of different temporal moments as a single prediction task and learned the predictions of varied moments simultaneously, which would encounter a main limitation: the learning of short-term predictions (referring to ``near-future'' prediction) could be hindered by the predictions of long-term (referring to ``far-future'' prediction) motions. In this paper, we develop a novel temporal continual learning framework called Continual Prior Compensation (CPC) to progressively train HMP models, in which we divide the prediction task of motions corresponding to varied temporal moments into several subtasks and train the model in a multi-stage manner. To mitigate the prior information forgetting in the progressive training, we further introduce a learnable random variable Prior Compensation Factor (PCF) to explicitly measure the prior knowledge loss. We theoretically show that the PCF can be efficiently learned together with the model parameters by minimizing a reasonable upper bound of the objective function. The proposed CPC is further enhanced to estimate the prior information loss for each subtask and a new framework called Continual Prior Compensation++ (CPC++) with Fine-Grained Prior Compensation Factor (FGPCF) is finally developed. Our CPC and CPC++ frameworks are quite flexible and can be easily integrated with different HMP backbone models and adapted to various datasets and applications. Extensive experiments on three HMP benchmark datasets using multiple SOTA HMP backbones (PGBIG, siMLPe, MotionMixer, and LTD) demonstrate the effectiveness and flexibility of our frameworks.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652)
*Woojun Lee,Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSSD将扩散过程本身的加噪改为具有时间相关性的过程：用稳定对角状态空间模型的阻尼振荡模态构造时间核，并以该模型平稳协方差的Cholesky因子作为时间算子，在不改变去噪器结构的前提下实现多时间尺度、振荡相关的时序噪声，在科学场与机器人视频数据集上优于共享去噪器的扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列扩散预测对每个未来帧施加独立噪声，时序依赖完全交给去噪器学习，加噪过程本身不含时间结构。作者希望直接在损坏（corruption）过程中编码时序结构。但直接使用状态空间模型存在难点：有限片段缺少首帧之前的状态，零状态初始化会使早期帧噪声低于扩散调度规定，破坏训练与采样一致性。

Method: 用稳定对角SSM的阻尼振荡模态构造时间核（学习衰减率与频率以捕捉多时间尺度与振荡相关）；以模型平稳协方差的Cholesky因子作为时间算子，替代未观测初始状态；去噪器网络结构不变，仅改为预测时间相关噪声，采样时每步去除并重新施加时间混合。理论上证明该算子是因果的、精确可逆、保持每帧噪声方差与标准扩散一致、在过程干净端退化为单位矩阵，且恰为该SSM的Kalman innovations形式。

Result: 在科学场数据集与机器人视频数据集上，DSSD优于共享相同去噪器的扩散基线；训练中学到的模态演化显示时间算子能随数据集自适应。

Conclusion: 将时序结构内化到加噪过程本身是可行且有效的，Cholesky平稳协方差算子为有限长度序列提供了方差精确、因果可逆的解决方案，并统一解释了其与Kalman innovations滤波的联系。

Abstract: Diffusion models for temporal prediction typically corrupt each future frame with independent noise, leaving temporal dependence entirely to the denoiser. We propose Dynamical State-Space Diffusion (DSSD), which learns the temporal structure of the corruption process itself. DSSD builds a temporal kernel from the damped oscillatory modes of a stable diagonal state-space model, whose learned decay rates and frequencies let the corruption capture multiple timescales and oscillatory correlations, while the denoiser architecture remains unchanged. Running this model on a finite clip, however, requires the state before the first frame, which is unobserved; starting from a zero state leaves the early frames with less noise than the diffusion schedule prescribes. We avoid this by using the Cholesky factor of the model's stationary covariance as the temporal operator. We prove that this operator is causal, exactly invertible, keeps each frame's noise variance equal to that of standard diffusion, and reduces to the identity at the clean end of the process, and that it is exactly the Kalman innovations form of the state-space model. The denoiser is trained to predict the temporally correlated noise, and sampling removes and reapplies the temporal mixing at each step. On scientific-field and robot-video datasets, DSSD outperforms diffusion baselines that share its denoiser, and the evolution of its learned modes during training shows that the temporal operator adapts to each dataset.

</details>


### [108] [CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664)
*Reza Akbari Movahed,Abuzar Rezaee,Shatha Alamri,Edmond S. L. Ho,Fani Deligianni,Ali Gooya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CardioDrift，一个全自动、基于图谱的概率框架，在具有全局对应点的 3D 图谱网格上表示个体心脏运动，将 CPD 推广为多区域联合配准并内嵌切片偏移校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏运动追踪方法分为两类：一类不具备跨受试者的解剖对应关系，限制了组间运动比较；另一类依赖全局对应点，但需要耗时且易出错的心肌图谱半自动拟合步骤。作者希望消除该半自动环节，实现全自动且解剖对应的运动表示。

Method: 1）将 Coherent Point Drift（CPD）概率配准框架推广，联合把多个心脏区域的图谱表面配准到从 cine CMR 分割图中提取的、跨心动周期解剖对应的轮廓；2）在同一概率模型内嵌入切片偏移（slice-shift）校正，估计逐层平移以抑制影响图谱拟合精度的错位伪影。

Result: 在随机选取的 1301 名 UK Biobank 受试者及 M&M 数据集上评估，LV、Myo、RV 的 Dice 相似系数分别为 92.23±8.70%、82.11±7.56%、88.69±4.13%，在两个数据集的所有距离与重叠指标上均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: CardioDrift 以全自动方式实现了具备全局解剖对应关系的心脏运动表示，避免了对半自动图谱拟合的依赖，并在大规模人群数据上取得一致优于基线的分割与配准精度，为跨受试者心肌运动量化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac motion tracking from cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging is essential for quantifying myocardial motion abnormalities associated with cardiovascular disease. Existing methods fall into two groups: those that represent motion without anatomical correspondence across subjects and therefore limit inter-subject motion assessment; and those that represent motion with globally corresponding points, which typically require a time-consuming and error-prone semi-automatic step to fit a cardiac atlas to the subject space. To address these limitations, this study proposes CardioDrift, a fully automatic, atlas-based probabilistic framework that represents subject-specific cardiac motion on a 3D atlas mesh with globally corresponding points. The Coherent Point Drift (CPD) formulation is generalised to jointly register the atlas surfaces of multiple cardiac regions to the anatomically corresponding contours extracted from cine CMR segmentation maps throughout the cardiac cycle. Slice-shift correction is embedded in the same probabilistic model, estimating slice-wise translations that suppress the misalignment artefact limiting atlas-fitting accuracy. Evaluation on 1301 randomly selected UK Biobank subjects and the M&M dataset, CardioDrift attains Dice similarity coefficients (DSCs) of $92.23\pm8.70\%$, $82.11\pm7.56\%$, and $88.69\pm4.13\%$ for the left ventricle (LV), myocardium (Myo), and right ventricle (RV), exceeding the strongest baseline on every distance and overlap metric of both datasets.

</details>


### [109] [Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685)
*Zhengtao Liu,Jinrong Xu,Shiu-Hong Kao,Huixin Sun,Yicong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出新任务GRVS及基准Isekai与在线框架Sekai，建模流式推理中目标集随事件演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线推理视频分割假定目标集在视频中固定，忽略了流式推理中随新事件出现、满足查询的对象可能随时间变化这一本质属性。

Method: 提出GRVS任务：目标集依赖已观测视频历史中累积的查询相关事件，可从空集演化为单个或多个对象。构建Isekai基准（60视频、197查询、11类时序查询、12,569个评分点，目标集具时序因果标注）。设计Sekai框架：事件记忆推理器（EMR）以结构化记忆维护查询相关事件与演化目标状态；记忆引导解码器（MGD）从记忆识别有效目标并在当前帧定位。

Result: Sekai显著优于现有在线RVS方法，达50.33 J&F，较最强基线提升3.2点。

Conclusion: 将目标有效性维护与当前帧定位解耦并借助结构化事件记忆，可有效应对目标集动态演化的流式推理分割；Isekai与Sekai为后续研究提供基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Online reasoning video segmentation (Online RVS) requires a model to segment query-referred objects as a video streams in, using only the frames observed so far. Existing formulations largely assume a fixed target set throughout the video, overlooking a fundamental property of streaming reasoning: as new events occur, the objects that satisfy a query may change over time. To address this, we propose a new task, Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation (GRVS), where the target set depends on query-relevant events accumulated over the observed video history. As new evidence accumulates, the target set can evolve from empty to one or multiple objects, requiring a model to continuously maintain target validity while localizing the valid objects currently in view. To evaluate GRVS, we build Isekai, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 videos and 197 queries across 11 temporal query families, with 12,569 scoring points whose target sets are causally annotated over time. We further propose Sekai, an online framework with two complementary components: an Event Memory Reasoner (EMR) that maintains query-relevant events and evolving target states in structured memory, and a Memory-Grounded Decoder (MGD) that identifies valid targets from this memory and localizes them in the current frame. Experiments show that Sekai significantly outperforms existing online RVS methods, achieving 50.33 J&F and improving over the strongest baseline by 3.2 points. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/zenastangbi/Sekai.

</details>


### [110] [Agent-Driven Generative Priors for Pathology Image Synthesis and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06701)
*Sicheng Yang,Rui Tang,Haipeng Zhou,Hongqiu Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Jianda Ma,Lie Dai,Guang Yang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架，整合自动数据筛选、生成式表征学习与下游任务高效适配，在病理图像语义分割上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理深度学习受限于大规模像素级标注的可获得性低、成本高。合成数据可缓解该问题，但现有流程缺乏可靠的质量控制，且与下游分析联系较弱。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 构建病理智能体（agent）系统评估图像质量并过滤样本，从公开TCGA库中筛选出TCGA-200K（217,030个patch）；(2) 在筛选数据上训练生成模型，其主干除图像合成外还学习可迁移的结构与语义表征；(3) 用轻量级任务专用头将预训练生成主干适配到语义分割。另构建PathSeg-380K，含约38万个细胞、12类细胞的专家标注语义分割数据集。

Result: 在多个病理数据集上，所提框架取得最先进的分割性能。

Conclusion: 通过智能体筛选保障数据质量、以生成模型主干提供可迁移表征、并以轻量头实现高效适配，可将合成数据生成与下游分析紧密衔接，并有效提升病理分割性能。

Abstract: Deep learning methods for pathology are constrained by the limited availability and high cost of large-scale pixel-level annotations. Synthetic data can alleviate this limitation, but existing pipelines often lack reliable quality control and remain weakly connected to downstream analysis. To address these issues, we propose a three-stage framework that integrates automated data screening, generative representation learning, and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. First, we develop a pathology agent system to assess image quality and filter samples, resulting in TCGA-200K, a dataset containing 217,030 pathology patches selected from the public TCGA repository. Second, we train a generative model on the screened data. In addition to image synthesis, its backbone learns structural and semantic representations that can be transferred to downstream tasks. Third, we adapt the pretrained generative backbone to semantic segmentation using a lightweight task-specific head. We further introduce PathSeg-380K, a semantic segmentation dataset with expert annotations for approximately 380,000 cells across 12 cell types. Experiments on multiple pathology datasets show that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/script-Yang/Agent-GenPath.

</details>


### [111] [Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728)
*Shuai Dong,Kanghui Tian,Linghan Chen,Wenqi Shao,Hongrui Wu,Haowen Hou,Yongfu Zhu,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Congcong Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Caoyuan Ma,Taiqiang Wu,Ji Zhao,Wenzheng Chang,Dianyi Wang,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei,Yu Qiao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TurnWise——面向流式视频理解的轮次级GRPO框架，通过轮级信用分配与轮均策略优化，使学习信号对齐每一次"回应/继续观察"的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需模型反复决策是否回应或继续观察。现有基于GRPO的方法侧重奖励设计，却沿用单一轨迹级优势，导致共享信用：早期成功后强化冗余回应，早期失败后抑制有用的后续回应，造成轨迹级反馈与轮级交互的错配。

Method: 两项互补设计：(1) 轮级信用分配——采用"静默补全前缀"打分（silence-completed prefix scoring），以每个决策的剩余回报构建组内相对优势，将其学习信号与先前奖励解耦；(2) 轮均策略优化——先在每轮内对token损失取平均，再跨轮平均，使回应与等待决策在输出长度不同时获得等权。无需学习型critic，也不增加额外rollout。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在流式视频理解任务上优于既有强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 将信用分配与损失归约对齐到轮级决策，可有效提升流式策略学习：促进有信息量的回应、减少冗余，且以轻量方式实现。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [112] [Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738)
*Zeno Nongmaithem,Anushttha Srivastava,Anshit Sinha,Apoorv Sisodia,Suman Chowdhury,Prerana Mukherjee,Rajesh P. Barnwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoupledSGA 通过关系交互图+图注意力耦合实体共享的多个关系预测，并引入基于互斥谓词矩阵的结构一致性损失；同时揭示 With-Constraint 评测协议无法捕捉组内互斥违规，建议补充 No-Constraint 结果与矛盾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间 SGA 中每个 subject-object 对用独立 Neural ODE 演化，共享实体的关系在积分过程中无法相互影响，且无法阻止同一对出现"坐在…上"与"站在…上"等互斥预测。

Method: 提出 CoupledSGA：用 Relationship Interaction Graph 将相关关系连接，在 ODE 向量场内加入图注意力实现耦合；并加入基于互斥谓词矩阵的结构一致性损失 L_SC。理论上分析 L_SC 的梯度只依赖谓词分数而非真值标签，并证明 With-Constraint 的 R@K/mR@K 在组内获胜谓词及其分数固定时不变。

Result: Action Genome 上：两轮预热后激活 L_SC，R@20 从 42.21% 降至 35.18%，No-Constraint 矛盾率从 45.92% 降至 4.07%；匹配预算下 R@20 为 CoupledSGA 40.48% vs. SceneSayerODE 44.78%。实验也验证了惩罚可能提升一致性却降低召回。

Conclusion: CoupledSGA 定位为测试平台而非 SOTA；With-Constraint 召回只能反映结构一致性约束的代价而非收益，建议对约束谓词共激活的方法同时报告 No-Constraint 结果与矛盾率。

Abstract: Scene Graph Anticipation (SGA) predicts object relationships in future video frames. Continuous-time SGA models evolve each subject-object pair with an independent Neural ODE, so relationships sharing an entity cannot influence one another during integration, and nothing prevents mutually exclusive predictions, such as "sitting on" and "standing on", for the same pair. We introduce CoupledSGA, which links such relationships through a Relationship Interaction Graph with graph attention inside the ODE vector field, and adds a Structural Consistency Loss ($\mathcal{L}_{SC}$) based on a matrix of mutually exclusive predicates.
  Developing CoupledSGA revealed a limitation of the evaluation protocol. With-Constraint evaluation keeps one predicate per group for each subject-object pair, so its R@K and mR@K cannot capture the within-group violations targeted by $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$: we prove they are unchanged whenever each group's winning predicate and its score are held fixed. We further show that the gradient of $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ depends on predicate scores rather than ground-truth labels: for two-predicate rules it amplifies the ordering the model already holds, and when all scores in a group are equal it suppresses predicates in more exclusion rules more strongly, explaining why the penalty can lower recall while improving consistency.
  On Action Genome, activating $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ after two warm-up epochs reduces R@20 from 42.21% to 35.18% while lowering the No-Constraint contradiction rate from 45.92% to 4.07% (matched-budget R@20: 40.48% CoupledSGA vs. 44.78% SceneSayerODE). CoupledSGA is a testbed, not a state-of-the-art claim: With-Constraint recall captures the cost of enforcing structural consistency but not the improvement. We recommend reporting No-Constraint results alongside a contradiction rate for methods that constrain predicate co-activation.

</details>


### [113] [TailSteer: Trajectory-Tail Steering for Drift-Resistant Multi-turn Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06741)
*Ruikang Li,Zixuan Chen,Jiarui Wu,Mingde Yao,Yiyuan Zhang,Yujin Wang,Hanming Deng,Lewei Lu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TailSteer——一种末段训练框架：保持早期去噪动态贴近基座模型，将对齐/保真修正集中在轨迹尾部，从编辑器自身的on-policy去噪rollout中学习（以未改动的源内容作为保真参考），从而在不重新学习既有能力的前提下提升多轮编辑的长程鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮编辑性能强并不代表多轮交互可靠：编辑输出被反复复用时，微小的非预期改动会逐步累积成严重的视觉漂移。现有缓解手段（多轮数据训练、跨轮rollout）可能改变预训练先验，而推理期专用机制难以扩展到基础模型。

Method: 最终阶段训练框架。原则：让早期去噪动力学接近基座模型，把保真修正集中在轨迹尾部（细粒度偏差更易被抑制之处）。修正项由编辑器自身的on-policy去噪rollout学习，以未修改的源内容作为保真参考，无需人工构造的ground-truth编辑配对。

Result: 在FLUX、SenseNova、Qwen系列上，15–20轮编辑中显著降低累积漂移，同时在大量文生图与单图编辑基准上基本保持基座模型性能。

Conclusion: 长程多轮编辑鲁棒性可在基础模型中通过TailSteer"解锁"，而非依赖重新学习其已有能力，兼顾鲁棒性与原始生成/编辑能力的保持。

Abstract: Strong single-turn editing performance does not guarantee reliable multi-turn interaction: as edited outputs are repeatedly reused, small unintended changes can accumulate into severe visual drift. This long-term drift is often mitigated through training on multi-turn data or cross-turn rollouts, potentially altering pretrained priors, or through specialized inference-time mechanisms that are difficult to scale to foundation models. We introduce TailSteer, a final-stage training framework that improves multi-turn robustness while retaining the generative and editing capabilities acquired through earlier training. TailSteer follows a simple principle: keep early denoising dynamics close to those of the base model and concentrate preservation corrections near the trajectory tail, where fine-grained deviations can be suppressed. These corrections are learned from the editor's own on-policy denoising rollouts, using unchanged source content as a preservation reference without requiring curated ground-truth editing pairs. Experiments across the FLUX, SenseNova, and Qwen families show that TailSteer substantially reduces cumulative drift over 15-20 editing turns while largely preserving base-model performance on extensive text-to-image and single-image editing benchmarks. These results demonstrate that long-horizon editing robustness can be unlocked in foundation models after TailSteer, rather than by relearning their existing capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [EvoWorld: Towards Out-of-Sight World Evolution in Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06748)
*Yequan Bie,Dengyang Jiang,Zhixuan Chen,Xiaozhuan Liang,Haoxuan Che,Quande Liu,Xi Chen,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution"新任务，并给出brain-renderer协作框架 EvoWorld 及 EvoWorld-Data/Bench，实现事件条件下的因果一致世界演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型被当作视觉世界模拟器使用，但其优势在于渲染看似合理的像素，而非推理世界应如何演化。当事件发生时，模型需先推断其对世界中实体的后果，再渲染结果状态，而现有扩散视频模型缺乏显式的世界状态维护与事件条件后果推理机制，常产生视觉合理但行为静止或因果不一致的输出。

Method: 将问题形式化为 Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution：要求模型渲染事件、推理其对演化中世界状态的影响，并在世界继续演化（甚至超出视野）时呈现因果合理的结果。作为基线参考方案，提出 EvoWorld——brain-renderer 协作框架：以视觉语言模型充当"world brain"，观察已生成片段、维护不断更新的世界状态并推理可能的状态转移；由分段自回归视频渲染器将其视觉化。同时构建 EvoWorld-Data 与 EvoWorld-Bench，用于训练与评测事件实现、因果反应与重访一致性。

Result: 实验表明，EvoWorld 在保持视觉质量与一致性的同时，相较近期模型显著提升了事件条件下的因果演化能力。

Conclusion: 为超越当前视野的、推理引导的自主世界演化建立了参考基准点，指出世界状态显式建模与因果推理是视频世界模拟器的关键缺口。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly used as visual world simulators, yet their primary strength lies in rendering plausible pixels rather than reasoning about how a world should evolve. Coherent world evolution requires more than visual fidelity: when an event occurs, a model must infer its consequences for the entities in the world and then render the resulting state. For example, after a wild beast appears, the model should not only depict the wild beast, but also infer whether a nearby person should flee, take cover, or fight back based on the realized scene. Existing diffusion-based video models lack an explicit mechanism for maintaining world state and reasoning over such event-conditioned consequences, often producing visually plausible but behaviorally static or causally inconsistent outputs. To make this reasoning gap concrete and measurable, we propose a new problem, Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution, which requires a model to render an event, reason about its consequences for the evolving world state, and reveal a causally plausible outcome as the world continues to evolve, even out of sight. As a baseline reference solution, we introduce EvoWorld, a brain-renderer collaboration framework in which a vision-language model serves as the world brain: it observes generated segments, maintains an evolving account of the world, and reasons about plausible state transitions, while a segment-autoregressive video renderer realizes them visually. We further introduce EvoWorld-Data and EvoWorld-Bench for training and evaluating event realization, causal reactions, and revisit consistency. Experiments show that EvoWorld substantially improves event-conditioned causal evolution over recent models while preserving visual quality and consistency, establishing a reference point for reasoning-guided, autonomous world evolution beyond the current field of view.

</details>


### [115] [Route First, See Later: Enabling Vision Agents to Reason Scientifically by Separating Routing Policy from Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06760)
*Shubhabrata Mukherjee,Jeffrey Donatelli,Daniela Ushizima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZETA 提出一种可扩展架构，将科学视觉智能体的"路由策略"与"动作执行"解耦，使路由正确性与下游执行可分别评估。在51条零样本科学意图查询上，显式路由策略语义显著提升路由准确率（Qwen 3.8 分割路由 6.2%→75.0%，检测路由 60.0%→100%），但增益因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像分析工作流要求智能体在描述、检测、计数、分割等多能力间选择工具。现有多工具架构将意图解释与视觉执行混为一谈，无法判断失败源于推理错误（误路由）还是感知失败（工具执行无效），缺乏可审计性，难以用于高风险科学自动化。

Method: ZETA 在调用专用视觉工具前先解析科学意图，把测量视为基于显式几何的确定性操作而非生成式输出；架构上将路由策略与固定合法动作接口分离。作者开展首个受控研究，在保持动作接口不变的前提下单独操控显式路由策略规范，并在51条科学意图查询的零样本基准上评估。

Result: 显式路由语义可大幅提升路由准确率，尤其在语义相邻的动作边界处；但改进幅度随模型而异。Qwen 3.8 分割路由由6.2%升至75.0%，检测路由由60.0%升至100%。执行轨迹使路由决策与下游产物可分别观测。

Conclusion: ZETA 为区分"意图选择错误"与"视觉执行失败"提供了基础设施，是部署可靠科学视觉智能体的关键前提；但其结论基于单一零样本基准，跨模型增益不一致，泛化性仍待验证。

Abstract: Scientific image-analysis workflows require agents to select among diverse capabilities like description, detection, counting, and segmentation. Current multi-tool architectures conflate intent interpretation with visual execution, masking whether a failure arises from flawed reasoning (misrouting) or failed perception (invalid tool execution). This lack of auditability renders agents unreliable for high-stakes scientific automation. We introduce ZETA, an extensible architecture that operationalizes the decoupling of routing policy from action execution. ZETA resolves scientific intent before invoking specialized visual tools, treating measurement as a deterministic operation grounded in explicit geometry rather than generative output. This architecture exposes the "black box" of agent decision-making, allowing routing correctness and downstream execution to be evaluated as independent failure modes.
  To our knowledge, we provide the first controlled study in scientific vision-agent routing that isolates explicit routing-policy specification while holding the legal action interface fixed. Evaluation on a zero-shot benchmark of 51 scientific-intent queries shows that explicit routing semantics can substantially improve routing accuracy, particularly at semantically adjacent action boundaries, although improvements vary across models. For Qwen 3.8, explicit routing-policy specification improves segmentation routing accuracy from 6.2% to 75.0% and detection routing accuracy from 60.0% to 100%. ZETA's execution traces make routing decisions and downstream artifacts separately observable, providing a basis for distinguishing intent-selection errors from visual execution failures. ZETA establishes the necessary infrastructure to isolate execution errors from interpretive ones, a critical requirement for deploying reliable scientific vision agents.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning for Remote Sensing in Urban Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06770)
*Jiawei Zhang,Rizhao Cai,Dayan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出细粒度城市遥感基准UMBench并揭示MLLM依赖场景先验的问题，进而提出免训练的证据优先思维链方法提升开源与商用MLLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市管理中的遥感视觉推理需要细粒度能力，但现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评测对此关注不足。

Method: 构建基于无人机图像的细粒度城市遥感基准UMBench，覆盖交通监控、城市建设、环境质量、灾害预警等任务；并提出免训练的证据优先思维链（evidence-first chain-of-thought）方法，引导模型优先检查细粒度视觉证据。

Result: 评测显示现有MLLM常依赖场景级先验而忽略细粒度视觉证据；所提方法在UMBench上同时提升开源与商用MLLM的性能。

Conclusion: UMBench填补了城市遥感细粒度推理评测空白，证据优先思维链为免训练增强MLLM细粒度推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual reasoning is essential for remote sensing in urban management, yet remains underexplored in the evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). We introduce UMBench, a fine-grained urban remote sensing benchmark built from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images for multiple urban management tasks such as traffic monitoring, urban construction, environmental quality, and disaster warning. Evaluations on UMBench reveal that current MLLMs often rely on scene-level priors and overlook fine-grained visual evidence. To address this issue, we further propose a novel evidence-first chain-of-thought method that directs models to inspect fine-grained visual evidence in a training-free manner. Experiments show that our method can improve the performance of both open-source and commercial MLLMs on UMBench.

</details>


### [117] [Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781)
*Chang Liu,Ke Han,Niccolò Biondi,Gloria Isotton,Elisa Ricci,Davide Talon,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义残差向量量化框架SRVQ，将手语生成拆分为"语义→运动学"两级过程：第一残差层编码手语内容，后续层精化姿态细节，单一量化器内实现，无需外部规划阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语生成需同时决定"打什么手语"（词汇内容）与"怎么打"（手型、轨迹、表情、身体协调）。现有时序姿态方法与仅重建的运动tokenizer将两者纠缠，生成器缺乏显式的文本对齐中间目标。

Method: 提出SRV Quantizer，学习分层残差码本：第1残差层编码语义内容，后续层精化pose、handshape、trajectory与body coordination，全在单一量化器内完成。配合词感知条件（word-aware conditioning）进行层级码生成：掩码语义生成器预测语义码，运动学生成器补全残差码，合成完整SMPL-X运动。评估采用共享文本-运动嵌入空间的检索与FID指标，并辅以标准几何指标。

Result: 在CSL-Daily与Phoenix-2014T上，R@1分别达82.3与70.3，较此前SOTA分别提升30与46个点。

Conclusion: 通过将语义与运动学解耦于统一的分层残差量化框架，SRVQ为手语生成提供了显式的文本对齐中间表示，显著提升检索对齐质量与生成保真度。

Abstract: Sign language generation (SLG) maps written sentences into signing motion while preserving both lexical content and fine-grained articulation. This requires a model to decide what signs should be expressed and to realize them through precise handshape, trajectory, facial expression and body coordination. Existing pose-based systems and reconstruction-only motion tokenizers often entangle these two decisions, leaving the generator without an explicit text-aligned intermediate target for what to sign before refining how to sign it. To address this issue, we introduce Semantic Residual Vector Quantization (SRVQ), a framework for SLG that frames generation as a two-level process moving from semantics to kinematics. The newly introduced SRV Quantizer learns a hierarchy in which the first residual level encodes sign content and subsequent levels refine pose, handshape, trajectory, and body coordination, all within a single quantizer and without separate semantic representations or external planning stages. Through word-aware conditioning for hierarchical code generation, a masked semantic generator predicts semantic codes and a kinematic generator completes the residual codes for full SMPL-X motion synthesis. We evaluate using retrieval and FID in a shared text-motion embedding space, alongside standard geometric metrics. On CSL-Daily and Phoenix-2014T, SRVQ achieves R@1 of 82.3 and 70.3, outperforming the previous SOTA by 30 and 46 points, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796)
*Yihong Luo,Tianyu Pang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出速度对抗蒸馏（VAD）及其轨迹分布匹配实现VA-TDM，通过学习式critic比较学生与教师的velocity field，为少步扩散/流生成模型提供更灵活的监督，在文生图与文生视频上取得四步采样的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏（four-step等）虽高效，但少步采样时学生中间步的score估计困难，常用估计器所刻画的分布与更新学生所用分布不一致，导致基于score distillation的分布匹配方法难以准确跟踪学生分布、质量受损。flow matching的velocity field监督更灵活，可绕开这一问题。

Method: 将监督从score重构为velocity field：用可学习critic比较学生与教师的velocity场，把二者分歧转为学习反馈，构成VAD；理论上VAD统一了score distillation、Fisher-based matching与moment matching，并解释了既有启发式MMD更新、导出新训练目标。进一步提出VA-TDM：沿学生轨迹做轨迹分布匹配（对部分去噪过程给监督），结合transition-aware在线velocity学习，以及在中间学生transition上的双形式VAD监督。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上，文生图与文生视频的四步生成均达SOTA；可有效扩展至33B的MiniMax-H3；VA-TDM-H3显著优于少步Fast-H3，按人类偏好更接近原始多步H3。

Conclusion: 以velocity场为核心重构分布匹配式蒸馏，能在极少采样步数下稳定优于score-based方法；VAD提供统一理论框架，VA-TDM给出可扩展、实用的少步生成训练方案。

Abstract: Few-step distillation offers efficient generation with diffusion and flow models, but preserving quality with only a few sampling steps remains challenging. Distribution-matching methods via score distillation provide a strong foundation, yet they rely on accurately tracking the student's changing distribution. At intermediate generation steps in few-step sampling, student-score estimation can be difficult, and commonly used estimators may describe a different distribution from the one used to update the student. Flow matching allows a more flexible approach to supervision through velocity fields constructed from the student's sampling process. Building on this perspective, we introduce Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD), which uses a learned critic to compare student and teacher fields and turn their disagreement into feedback for learning. VAD unifies score distillation, Fisher-based matching, and moment matching, also explaining existing heuristic MMD updates and delivering new training objectives. We further develop VA-TDM, which follows trajectory distribution matching: providing supervision for partial denoising along the student trajectory. Beyond this, VA-TDM combines transition-aware online velocity learning and dual-form VAD supervision across intermediate student transitions. Extensive results show that VA-TDM is powerful for learning few-step generators, achieving state-of-the-art four-step performance in both text-to-image and text-to-video generation across various backbones and scaling effectively to 33B MiniMax-H3. In particular, our VA-TDM-H3 notably outperforms the few-step Fast-H3 and is closer to the original many-step H3 according to human preference.

</details>


### [119] [Open-HumanScale: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817)
*Juncheng Ma,Jiaxin Li,Jiankai Tu,Bo Liang,Guodong Lv,Yufan Deng,Junzhe Tang,Liangzhu Yang,Chen Bian,Zicong Cao,Baohao Zhu,Jianxin Bi,Tengfei Yan,Yinyuan Zhang,Kewei Zhang,Xinglong Chen,Kaiqi Chen,Duomin Wang,Xiaotian Tang,Liang Tan,Li Li,Yuan Xu,Haosen Yang,Yuqi Wang,Jingfei Xia,Songyan Huang,Zhiyang Dou,Zhen Dong,Enze Xie,Tat-Seng Chua,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Open-HumanScale 全开放配方，将第一人称人类视频预训练扩展至 5 万小时，并揭示数据量与机器人成功率间的对数线性缩放律，进而构建 HumanWAM 系列世界-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类视频是可扩展的操作先验来源，但原始数据规模本身并不等价于可泛化的机器人策略，缺乏严谨的数据筛选与训练配方，也缺乏对"纯人类数据预训练"缩放规律的实证理解。

Method: 构建 Open-HumanScale-50K 语料（带手部运动与自然语言密集标注）及配套数据筛选、预训练流水线；通过闭环仿真评测系统研究纯人类预训练；提出仅用 5 万小时人类视频预训练的 HumanWAM-Vanilla，以及视觉目标条件化的联合人-机预训练模型 HumanWAM-Goal。

Result: 发现预训练数据小时数与下游机器人成功率呈经验对数线性缩放律。HumanWAM-Vanilla 后训练后达 RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean 92.08%、Clean2Random 68.88%，RoboDojo 平均成功率 14.17%、平均进度 19.00。HumanWAM-Goal 借助混合数据缩放刷新 SOTA：Clean2Clean 92.70%、Clean2Random 84.60%；在实机上展现无需参数更新的涌现式具身上下文学习。

Conclusion: 人类第一人称视频预训练具备可预测的缩放效益，人-机混合数据可显著提升分布外泛化与零样本迁移；开源语料、流水线、检查点与评测套件以支持可复现的规模化具身智能研究。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanScale, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanScale-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanScale-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

</details>


### [120] [Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838)
*Jixuan Ying,Haoyu Liu,Timing Yang,Tingyu Zhu,Predrag Neskovic,Anand Bhattad,Cihang Xie,Zeyu Zheng,Alan Yuille,Feng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出上下文感知的自回归图像生成框架，模型根据全局上下文与当前局部信息动态决定下一个 token 的位置，而非固定或随机顺序，从而提升结构鲁棒性与推理感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归图像生成的生成顺序通常是预设或启发式的（如逐 patch 预测、随机顺序预测、特定尺度下的一步生成），这些策略会使模型忽视图像中重要的结构依赖关系，限制结构鲁棒性与推理能力。生成顺序被视为关键但研究不足的因素。

Method: 构建上下文感知的自回归生成框架，让生成过程不再遵循固定或随机顺序，而是依据全局上下文与当前局部信息动态确定下一个待生成 token 的空间位置，使生成顺序自适应于图像结构与语义依赖。方法可适用于多种 AR 范式，包括掩码自回归与尺度维自回归模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，上下文感知生成在不同图像 AR 范式（掩码自回归、尺度维自回归）下均显著提升结构鲁棒性；同时观察到涌现的推理特性：模型能更好理解复杂语义指令，生成的图像更忠实地遵循给定引导。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是图像自回归建模中关键但尚未充分探索的因素，上下文感知解码可解锁更强的推理感知视觉生成能力。代码见 https://github.com/Hpesojyjx/Saccades-AR。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generation has recently emerged as a competitive visual generation paradigm, yet its generation order is often predefined or heuristic, such as next-patch prediction, random-order prediction, or one-step generation for specific scales. We observe that these strategies can cause models to overlook important structural dependencies in images, thereby limiting both structural robustness and reasoning capability. In this work, we propose a context-aware autoregressive generation framework, where the model dynamically determines the next token location based on global context and current local information. Rather than following a fixed or random order, our method allows the generation process to adapt to image structure and semantic dependencies. Extensive experiments show that context-aware generation significantly improves structural robustness across different image AR paradigms, including masked autoregressive and scale-wise autoregressive models. Beyond quantitative improvements, we further observe emerging reasoning properties: the model can better understand complex semantic instructions and generate images that more faithfully follow the given guidance. These findings suggest that generation order is a critical yet underexplored factor in image autoregressive modeling, and that context-aware decoding can unlock stronger reasoning-aware visual generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/Hpesojyjx/Saccades-AR.

</details>


### [121] [Same Layout, Different Count: What Number Words Do Past Ten in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06840)
*Liang Yue,Eric A. Vance*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了5个开源文生图模型在物体数量较大（约10以上）时对数字词的遵循能力，通过控制物体、初始噪声等变量并让3,088张图像经历人类计数，揭示了各模型的"数量遵循上限"（SD3.5-M与FLUX.1-dev约10，FLUX.2[klein]与Qwen-Image约25），并指出初始噪声的粗粒度成分决定构图而非数量、数字词虽进入去噪器但缺乏明确方向性，最终提出以固定噪声的对比测试来区分"松散遵循"与"完全忽略"数字的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型在生成小数量（约10以内）物体时能遵循提示中的数字词，但超过一定规模后行为不明。论文希望系统刻画"数字词"在大数量场景下的实际作用边界：模型从何处开始失效、失效后构图与数量分别由什么决定、数字词是否仍影响去噪过程，以及现有评测指标为何可能误导。

Method: 在5个开源T2I模型上做受控实验：固定物体与初始噪声，逐次只改变一个变量（如数字词、噪声粒度）。引入"reach（遵循上限）"概念衡量模型遵循数字的最大规模。通过只重绘比1/8图像更细的噪声来测试粗粒度噪声对构图与数量的分别作用；分析数字词对去噪器早期步骤的影响；比较"仅告知数字"与"在初始潜变量中以加噪网格呈现数量"两种条件；并收集3,088张图像由未见提示的人类进行计数；最后对比精确匹配、相对误差等指标，提出同噪声测试。

Result: 各模型仅在小规模下遵循数字，存在明确reach：SD3.5-M与FLUX.1-dev在略高于10后即失效，FLUX.2[klein]与Qwen-Image可松散遵循至25。超出reach后，粗粒度初始噪声固定构图但不决定数量，仅重绘细粒度噪声与更换数字词对布局的影响相当，且已使数量偏移约三分之二。数字词仍传入去噪器并在最初几步起作用，但未观察到明确方向。以加噪网格形式呈现数字时，人类在59%图像中能数出该数量，而仅在文本中告知时为4%。精确匹配分数难以区分松散遵循与忽略数字的模型，相对误差甚至可能偏好常数猜测16，而同噪声测试可有效区分。

Conclusion: 大数量下数字词的语义控制显著减弱，数量主要由噪声与采样随机性决定，文本数字缺乏稳定的方向性作用。常规精确匹配或相对误差指标不足以评估数量遵循能力，应采用固定同噪声、每个物体多次请求的对比测试来可靠区分模型是否真正遵循数字。

Abstract: Ask SD3.5-Medium for twelve of an object and then, from the same starting noise, for twenty-five. The picture keeps its layout, but the count changes about as much as it would under a new random seed, and in no consistent direction. We study what number words do once a request passes about ten objects, in five open text-to-image models. Holding the object and the starting noise fixed, we change one thing at a time, and people who never see the request count 3,088 of the images. Every model follows the number for small crowds, but only up to a point, its reach: SD3.5-M and FLUX.1-dev stop following it soon after ten, while FLUX.2 [klein] and Qwen-Image follow it loosely up to twenty-five. Beyond SD3.5-M's reach, the coarse part of the starting noise fixes the composition of the picture but not how many objects it holds: redrawing only the noise finer than an eighth of the image keeps the layout as well as a new number does, and already moves the count two-thirds as far. The number word still reaches the denoiser and acts in its first steps, but nothing we tried gives it a clear direction. Shown instead as a noised grid in the starting latent, the count is drawn: people find it in 59% of images, against 4% when it is only told. Past ten, an exact-match score barely separates a model that follows the number loosely from one that ignores it, and a relative-error score can even prefer a constant guess of sixteen. A same-noise test with a few requests per object tells them apart.

</details>


### [122] [Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844)
*Yingying Yan,Jiaqi Tang,Wei Wei,Qianzhou Wang,Lei Zhang,Jianmin Chen,Yuyang Xia,Botong Geng,Jinjian Wu,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DGP（Dynamic Gated Pathways），在ViT每一层与LLM每一层之间建立全连接路由，由每个LLM层依据自身语义状态动态门控其对各视觉层特征的偏好，实现自适应跨层视觉-语言交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流MLLM将单一固定层视觉表征注入LLM，并把所有LLM层视为统一消费者，忽视了不同LLM层对视觉粒度需求不同这一事实；现有多层融合方法或在编码器侧压缩层级特征，或依赖预定义的稀疏/层级连接，未能建模ViT与LLM层级间的自适应跨层交互。

Method: 提出Dynamic Gated Pathways路由机制：对所有ViT层与所有LLM层建立all-to-all连接；每个LLM层以自身语义状态为条件，动态生成对各视觉层特征的门控权重，从而按需选择视觉粒度。基于LLaVA-1.5-7B与13B实现。

Result: 相对LLaVA-1.5-7B基线取得一致提升（SQA^I +4.2、MM-Vet +3.3、VizWiz +3.1、LLaVA^W +3.1），并在多数基准上超越既有多次层视觉融合方法。逐层路由分析显示不同LLM层确实会按需选择不同的视觉粒度。

Conclusion: 动态门控全连接路由能够有效且可解释地建模ViT与LLM层级间的自适应交互，"在哪里连接"应作为MLLM设计中的显式可学习要素。

Abstract: A central yet under-explored question in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is where to connect---how visual information from a hierarchical vision encoder should be wired into the layer-wise semantics of a large language model. Mainstream MLLMs inject a fixed single-layer visual representation into the LLM and treat all LLM layers as a single consumer, ignoring that different layers demand different visual granularities. Recent multi-layer fusion methods either compress hierarchical features on the encoder side or rely on predefined sparse or hierarchical layer-to-layer connections, and therefore stop short of modeling adaptive cross-layer interactions between the ViT and LLM hierarchies. We address this gap with DGP (Dynamic Gated Pathways), a routing mechanism that establishes all-to-all connections between every ViT layer and every LLM layer, with each LLM layer dynamically gating its preference over visual layers conditioned on its own semantic state. Built on top of LLaVA-1.5-7B and 13B, DGP delivers consistent gains over the LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline (e.g., +4.2 on SQA$^{I}$, +3.3 on MM-Vet, +3.1 on VizWiz and LLaVA$^{W}$) and surpasses prior multi-layer visual fusion methods on most benchmarks. Layer-wise routing analyses further show that different LLM layers do select different visual granularities on demand, supporting both the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed dynamic gated pathways. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/DGP.

</details>


### [123] [Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849)
*Hanyu Chen,Noah Snavely*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Trainspotting 数据集（12,068 个对象、311,886 张来自 Wikimedia Commons 的“found photos”），并揭示现有前馈重建模型在跨环境、跨物理实例对象上严重退化；用其微调 VGGT-Ω 可显著提升位姿、深度与几何质量，且在 NAVI 基准上可泛化，常规基准性能基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型主要在单次会话采集（single-session）数据上训练：视角变化但对象与环境基本固定。而公共照片档案中的“found photos”是多人在多年、多环境、多相机乃至多个物理实例下独立拍摄的，属于不同分布的多视图数据。作者希望填补这一数据与评测空白，检验并提升模型在此类 in-the-wild 对象中心场景下的重建能力。

Method: 构建 Trainspotting 数据集：12,068 个对象、311,886 张来自 Wikimedia Commons 的照片；对每个对象重建恢复相机位姿、多视图立体（MVS）深度与对象掩码。将对象划分为三种设定：(1) 固定于同一地点的对象；(2) 在多地之间移动的对象；(3) 由多个物理实例共享的同一设计。在此数据上评测近期前馈重建模型，并以 Trainspotting 微调 VGGT-Ω。

Result: 近期前馈重建模型在 Trainspotting 上表现不佳，后两种设定（跨地点移动对象、多物理实例共享设计）退化最严重。微调 VGGT-Ω 后在留出对象上的相机位姿、深度与 3D 几何显著提升；在独立的 NAVI in-the-wild 基准（每对象处于不同环境）上也观察到类似提升；在常规基准上性能基本保持不变。

Conclusion: “found photos”式多视图数据构成一个独立且更具挑战性的重建设定，现有单会话训练范式难以覆盖；以 Trainspotting 微调可有效迁移并泛化至其他 in-the-wild 基准，且不牺牲传统基准性能，说明数据分布而非模型架构是当前瓶颈之一。

Abstract: State-of-the-art feedforward reconstruction models perform poorly on in-the-wild object-centric photo collections. They are trained largely on single-session object captures whose images vary in viewpoint while the object and its environment remain nearly fixed. Public photo archives contain a different form of multi-view data. They hold found photos of the same object or design: photos taken independently by many people across years, environments, cameras, and sometimes physical units. In this work, we introduce Trainspotting, a dataset of 12,068 objects and 311,886 photos from Wikimedia Commons. We reconstruct each object to recover camera poses, multi-view stereo depth, and object masks. We categorize the objects into three regimes: objects fixed at one site, objects that move between sites, and designs shared by many physical units. Evaluating recent feedforward reconstruction models on Trainspotting shows that they fail most severely in the last two regimes. Fine-tuning VGGT-$Ω$ on Trainspotting significantly improves camera pose, depth, and 3D geometry on held-out objects. We observe similar improvements on the independent NAVI in-the-wild benchmark, which contains photos of each object in different environments. Performance on conventional benchmarks remains largely unchanged.

</details>


### [124] [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉Token路由（VTR）联合训练框架，学习保留动作预测所需信息的稀疏视觉表示，在保持性能的同时减少2.5-5.8倍视觉Token，降低推理成本至多3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）虽在长程基准上超越人类，但每张截图被表示为数千个视觉Token，随交互历史累积形成长上下文，导致计算成本高昂，限制其在长程任务与实时领域（如电子游戏）中的应用。作者观察到多数视觉Token要么与下一步动作语义无关，要么在相邻截图间存在时间冗余。

Method: VTR将视觉语言模型与轻量级时空路由器联合训练。路由器检查当前与上一帧截图中每个图像区域，判断其视觉Token是否进入语言模型，从而学习稀疏视觉表示；并采用自适应损失优化路由器，在尽可能减少视觉Token数量的同时最小化性能退化。

Result: 仅用2,000步训练，VTR在OSWorld、CUA-World及视频游戏动作预测上匹配全分辨率模型性能，视觉Token减少2.5-5.8倍；推理成本降低至多3.3倍，预填充速度提升至多2.9倍；在OSWorld上比降分辨率及其他丢弃Token的基线高出至多12.8分。

Conclusion: VTR使CUA仅处理所需的视觉Token，以极低计算成本支持长程任务与实时应用，为高效视觉表示学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently achieved superhuman performance on several long-horizon benchmarks. However, their computational cost limits their use in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains such as video games. A key bottleneck is representing each screenshot as thousands of visual tokens, which accumulate into long contexts as agents reason over extended interaction histories. We observe that most of these visual tokens are either semantically irrelevant to the agent's next action or temporally redundant across screenshots. Building on this observation, we introduce Visual Token Routing (VTR), a joint training framework for learning sparse visual representations that preserve the information needed for action prediction. In VTR, we train the vision-language model together with a lightweight spatio-temporal router that examines each image region in the current and previous screenshots and decides whether its visual token enters the language model. We optimize the router using an adaptive loss to keep the number of visual tokens as small as possible while minimizing performance degradation. With just 2,000 training steps, VTR matches the performance of the full-resolution model on OSWorld, CUA-World, and video game action prediction, while using 2.5-5.8x fewer visual tokens. These savings reduce inference cost by up to 3.3x and speed up prefill by up to 2.9x. VTR also outperforms lowering the image resolution and other baselines for dropping tokens, by up to 12.8 points on OSWorld. Overall, VTR enables computer-use agents to process only the visual tokens they need, allowing them to be used in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and models are available at https://pranjal2041.github.io/vtr

</details>


### [125] [LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856)
*Haoxiang Cao,Yuxuan Zhang,Penghui Du,Bo Li,Jiajiong Cao,Yingying Fan,Zimeng Wu,Yifu Luo,Mingzhe Zheng,Junwen Miao,Huaisong Zhang,Yang Yao,Yu Wang,Dongfu Jiang,Ping Nie,Wenhu Chen,Chaoqun Wang,Changqian Yu,Kelsey R. Allen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LongBanana：首个专家设计并验证的长上下文多参考图生成 benchmark，含 90 个任务、4,670 条原子评测项，并提出 LongBanana-Gen 智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体驱动的图像生成时代，缺少能够可审计地衡量"长上下文、多参考图、密集视觉证据使用"能力的评测平台。该任务需要检索分散的视觉证据、拒斥干扰项、绑定实体与关系，并把多张参考图合成为一张连贯图像，现有 benchmark 难以覆盖。

Method: 1) 按 3×3 分类法组织 90 个专家设计任务，每任务约 32 张参考图、20 条约束、50 个评测项，共 4,670 个专家指定的原子评测项；2) 采用图接地（graph-grounded）评测：为每个任务构建实体-关系图，将节点、边、全图三层义务转化为清单项，分别对应内容保真、关系绑定、全局约束满足；3) 质量保障：15% 双标注子集，精确一致率 89.4%，二次加权 kappa 0.83；4) 提出 LongBanana-Gen 智能体框架，含 ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) 工作流。

Result: 实验表明：最小化智能体外壳（minimal agent harness）已优于直接生成；RPR 工作流通过强化证据接地与修复，带来依赖于外壳设计的增益（harness-dependent gains）。

Conclusion: LongBanana 为测量密集视觉证据使用提供了可审计的测试平台，并为发展更强的智能体驱动图像生成系统提供了共同目标；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.

</details>


### [126] [CounterFactual Retrieval for Evidence-Aware Individual Animal Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06864)
*Ankit K. Upadhyay,Aleksandr Algasov,Ekaterina Nepovinnykh,Fedor Zolotarev,Tuomas Eerola,Heikki Kälviäinen,Charles V. Stewart,Lukas Picek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出**反事实前景检索（Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval, CFR）**协议，通过在完整图像、仅前景、仅捷径（背景/轮廓）三种视图下评估同一查询-图库检索任务，将单一检索分数转化为"证据画像"，以判定模型是否依赖身份相关的捷径而非动物本体特征；并发布约 50 万张掩码（覆盖 34 个数据集）及掩码生成与评估代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物个体识别正从精选小规模研究走向共享基准与可复用基础模型，但现有评估只给出检索准确率，无法说明匹配证据来源：高精度并不能证明模型使用了动物特征，还是利用了与身份相关的背景、轮廓等捷径信号。因此需要一个证据感知的评估范式。

Method: 提出 CFR 评估协议：对同一 query-gallery 检索任务构造三种视图——(1) 完整图像，衡量标准检索性能；(2) 仅前景，衡量前景支撑的检索能力；(3) 仅捷径（背景或轮廓），衡量在动物被移除后仍可从背景/轮廓恢复的身份信号量。三者共同构成证据画像。为支撑该协议，构建并发布 34 个数据集约 0.5M 前景掩码，并提供掩码生成与 CFR 评估代码。

Result: 以三视图指标组合将检索分数解构为证据画像：原始图像上的性能、仅保留动物时性能的保留比例，以及移除动物后背景/轮廓中仍可恢复的身份信号强度。同时产出覆盖 34 个数据集、约 0.5M 掩码的公开资源与开源工具链。

Conclusion: CFR 将评估从"匹配是否成功"推进到"匹配依据何种证据"，可揭示模型对捷径的依赖，为动物个体识别基准与基础模型提供更可信、可解释的评价标准；配套掩码与代码降低该协议的使用门槛，促进可复用的标准化评估。

Abstract: Automated identification of individual animals is moving from small curated studies toward shared benchmarks and reusable foundation models. This progress is useful, but current evaluation leaves the evidence behind a match unresolved, i.e., high retrieval accuracy does not show whether a model used animal features or identity-correlated shortcuts. We address this with Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval (CFR), an evidence-aware evaluation protocol. CFR evaluates the same query-gallery task under different views: full image, foreground-only, and shortcut-only. The full-image measures standard retrieval performance; the foreground-only measures foreground-supported retrieval; and shortcut-only view measure how much identity signal is recoverable from background, or silhouette. This turns a retrieval score into an evidence profile: how well the model performs on the original images, how much of that performance remains when only the animal is visible, and how much identity signal remains recoverable when the animal is removed. We release around 0.5M masks for 34 datasets and code for mask generation and CFR evaluation.

</details>


### [127] [PixelSpline: Continuous Space-Time Video Super-Resolution via Endpoint-Anchored Shared Pixel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06870)
*Wanqi Yuan,Nianyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续时空视频超分框架，结合长上下文编码、连续HR特征提升与端点锚定的PixelSpline，用共享的单一连续像素轨迹完成端点色彩与HR特征对齐，在质量-效率间取得更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时空视频超分仅依赖两个端点帧推断运动，且对两个运动方向独立建模，易受对应关系歧义与轨迹不一致的影响。

Method: 长上下文编码提供更可靠的端点表征；连续上采样直接在目标HR网格上构建细节外观特征；PixelSpline将每像素运动建模为两端点共享的单一连续轨迹，据此将端点颜色warp为粗中间帧并对其HR特征做残差重建。

Result: 在多种空间与时间上采样设置下的实验表明，该框架在质量与效率之间具有较优的权衡。

Conclusion: 以单一连续轨迹替代双向独立运动建模、并辅以长时上下文与HR网格上的连续特征提升，可有效缓解对应歧义与轨迹不一致问题，实现高效的高质量连续时空超分。

Abstract: Existing continuous space-time video super-resolution methods often infer motion from only two endpoint frames and model the two motion directions independently, making them vulnerable to ambiguous correspondence and inconsistent trajectories. We introduce a continuous STVSR framework that combines long-context encoding, continuous HR feature lifting, and an endpoint-anchored PixelSpline. Long-range observations provide more reliable endpoint representations, while continuous upsampling constructs detailed appearance features directly on the requested HR grid. PixelSpline explicitly models each pixel's motion as a single continuous trajectory shared by both endpoints. Using this trajectory, our method warps endpoint colors into a coarse intermediate frame and aligns the HR features for residual reconstruction. Experiments across spatial and temporal upsampling settings demonstrate that the proposed framework offers a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [128] [Procedural Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890)
*Jack Nugent,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出程序化对抗学习框架PAL及Simple-PAT算法，在程序生成器的低维控制参数空间中用零阶优化按需搜索困难样本，并构建PAL-Dojo基准，显著降低最坏情况误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在视觉任务上平均误差低，但在看似简单的场景中仍会失效；这些失效样本未必存在于固定数据集中，因此需要主动搜索困难实例以定位并减少失败。

Method: 将问题建模为minimax训练与对抗评估的程序化对抗学习框架：程序生成器是可解释程序，将低维控制参数（布局、光照、姿态等）映射为渲染图像及标签；Simple-PAT在生成器参数空间中采用零阶优化，按需提出对抗训练样本；同时构建PAL-Dojo，包含面向表面法线估计、图像分割与人体姿态估计的高效程序生成器。

Result: 在跨模型黑盒对手、总计5000万次查询的设定下，Simple-PAT将最坏情况误差分别较标准训练降低3.15倍、较最强鲁棒训练基线降低2.63倍（几何平均）。

Conclusion: 在可解释程序生成器的低维参数空间中做对抗搜索与训练，可有效发现并缓解固定数据集难以覆盖的失效模式，为鲁棒性评估与训练提供可控、可解释的替代范式。

Abstract: For many computer vision tasks, neural networks can be trained to produce a low average error, yet they still fail on seemingly simple scenes. Reducing these failures requires first finding them, but they may not be in a fixed dataset. We study procedural adversarial learning (PAL), a framework of minimax training and adversarial evaluation which searches the input space of a procedural generator for difficult instances. The procedural generator is an interpretable program mapping a low-dimensional vector of control parameters (e.g. layout, lighting, pose) to a rendered image and its label. We introduce the Simple-PAT algorithm, which uses zeroth-order optimization in the procedural generator's parameter space to propose adversarial training samples that are generated on demand. We also create PAL-Dojo, a collection of efficient procedural generators for surface normal estimation, image segmentation, and human pose estimation. Against a cross-model black-box adversary with 50M aggregate queries, Simple-PAT reduces worst-case error by a geometric mean of 3.15x compared to standard training and 2.63x compared to the strongest robust training baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/princeton-vl/procedural-adversarial-learning.

</details>


### [129] [Occlusion for Free: Detecting Occlusion from the Disagreement Between Two Monocular Distance Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06899)
*Chibueze Praise Onyenweaku,Toyibat Adele,Sikirulahi Opeyemi Abdulkareem,Obed Honour Eje*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、无需遮挡标注的遮挡评分：仅用相机管线已产生的表观高度与单目度量深度的排序关系即可读出遮挡，KITTI AUC 0.909，CARLA 合成场景 0.819。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要知道行人是否被遮挡，即使检测器无法恢复被遮挡像素；但遮挡估计器通常在部署中难以获得的遮挡标注上训练（跨域场景如拉各斯街头尤其缺乏标注），因此需要不依赖局部标注的遮挡信号。

Method: 利用两个现有距离估计：检测框的表观高度（遮挡下基本不变，因为框仍跨越整个人）与单目度量深度（被拉向遮挡物，因为框内最近表面被深度图报告）。仅使用二者的排序关系构造遮挡分数，无需焦距、身高或深度尺度，只需深度模型误差近似为纯增益（增益不变性），该条件可在无标签下检验。另测试了深度融合、遮挡模拟增强、注意力三种架构应对遮挡的表现作对照。

Result: KITTI 重度遮挡检测 AUC 0.909（全部标注行人 0.888，含检测器漏检者）；固定距离带内 AUC 0.894–0.925，而真实距离单独接近随机；CARLA 有真实可见度标注场景达 0.819（距离单独为 0.644）。三种标准架构改进在训练分布外均未提升检测；德国道路训练的检测器在尼日利亚拉各斯自标注街景上精度下降一半。

Conclusion: 遮挡信息可免费地从已有相机输出中读出，且非距离代理；论文强调在基准未覆盖的部署环境中，无需本地标注的信号更有价值。

Abstract: Knowing that a pedestrian is occluded is useful to a robot even when the detector cannot recover the hidden pixels, but occlusion estimators are normally trained on occlusion annotations that deployment settings rarely have. We show that occlusion can be read off two distance estimates a camera-only pipeline already computes. Apparent height is largely unchanged by occlusion, because the detected box still spans the whole person; monocular metric depth is pulled toward the occluder, because the nearest surface inside the box is what a depth map reports. Only their ordering matters, so no focal length, stature, or depth scale is needed, provided the depth model's error is close to a pure gain, a condition that can be checked without labels. The score detects heavy occlusion on KITTI with AUC 0.909 (0.888 over all annotated pedestrians, including those the detector misses), requires no training and no occlusion labels, and is not a proxy for range: within fixed distance bands it holds AUC 0.894-0.925 while true distance alone sits near chance. On synthetic CARLA scenes with measured visibility it reaches 0.819, against 0.644 for distance alone. We arrived here after measuring three standard architectural responses to occlusion, depth fusion, occlusion-simulating augmentation, and attention, none of which improved detection outside its training distribution in the configurations we tested, and after finding that a detector trained on German roads loses half its accuracy on self-annotated street scenes in Lagos, Nigeria. Signals that need no local annotation are worth more in settings that benchmarks do not represent.

</details>


### [130] [Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901)
*Yiwen Chen,Qiqi Hou,Jisoo Jeong,Yinhao Zhu,Huaizu Jiang,Amir Said*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文指出JEPA式世界模型存在"静态坍缩"失效模式——编码器保留回合专属静态内容而抑制操作相关状态变化，并提出动作引导的对比正则化（以光流相似度为软对比目标）来修正，在随机背景OGBench-Cube上把规划成功率从52%提升至78%，在LIBERO-Object上从14%提升至54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式世界模型仅在潜空间做动作条件动态预测而不重构观测，但单纯分布正则（如SIGReg）无法保证表征保留控制所需信息。作者识别出"静态坍缩"：静态内容在时间上可预测、但跨回合多样，因此即便有SIGReg也能长期存在；受控实验显示，仅引入随机背景（操作任务本身不变）就显著损害规划性能，说明表征被静态背景身份主导。

Method: 提出运动引导的对比正则化：将预测的潜状态转移与同回合候选观测的转移进行对比，降低静态背景身份在区分动作结果中的可用性；用光流相似度构造软对比目标，使运动相似的候选获得相似监督；该目标与潜预测损失、SIGReg联合优化，促使表征保留回合内状态区分度及相近转移间的相似性。

Result: 在含随机背景的OGBench-Cube上，规划成功率相对LeWorldModel（LeWM）从52%提升到78%；在LIBERO-Object上从14%提升到54%；在另外四个视觉控制基准上性能保持相当。

Conclusion: 分布正则不足以防止JEPA世界模型的静态坍缩，需显式引入以运动为监督信号的对比约束，才能让潜表征聚焦于控制相关状态变化，并在背景干扰下保持稳健的规划能力。

Abstract: JEPA-style world models learn action-conditioned latent dynamics without reconstructing observations, but distributional regularization alone does not ensure that their representations preserve information needed for control. We identify static collapse, a failure mode in which the encoder preserves episode-specific static content while suppressing manipulation-relevant state changes. Because static content is temporally predictable yet diverse across episodes, this failure can persist despite regularizers such as SIGReg. A controlled study on OGBench-Cube shows that introducing randomized backgrounds substantially degrades planning performance while leaving the underlying manipulation task unchanged. To address this problem, we propose motion-guided contrastive regularization. Our approach compares predicted latent transitions with transitions to candidate observations from the same episode, reducing the usefulness of static background identity for distinguishing action outcomes. Optical-flow similarities provide soft contrastive targets, allowing candidates with similar motion to receive similar supervision. Combined with latent prediction and SIGReg, our objective encourages representations to preserve within-episode state distinctions and similarities between related transitions. Our method improves planning success over LeWorldModel (LeWM) from 52% to 78% on OGBench-Cube with randomized backgrounds and from 14% to 54% on LIBERO-Object, while maintaining comparable performance on four other visual-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918)
*Weichen Fan,Haiwen Diao,Penghao Wu,Siyuan Hu,Ziqi Huang,Tianqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Ziyi Wang,Zhongang Cai,Hanming Deng,Quan Wang,Lei Yang,Lewei Lu,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无视觉编码器的VLA框架NEO-VLA，让图像与语言在骨干网络中联合演化，动作专家可从不同深度的指令条件化视觉表征中取用信息，实现更强、更鲁棒的操控控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流VLA模型基于"先由视觉编码器压缩感知、再开始动作推理"的前提，但操控往往依赖最易被这种抽象破坏的视觉信号（局部几何、边界、深度、细微状态变化）。由此提出核心问题：动作策略应继承固定的视觉终点，还是直接访问不断演化的视觉计算？

Method: 构建结构化无编码器的VLA框架：图像通过轻量patch卷积层token化，在骨干网络中与语言联合演化；流匹配（flow-matching）动作专家可从不同深度的指令条件化视觉表征中提取信息。采用受控对比实验、全量微调实验、规模扩展实验与逐层探针分析。

Result: 受控对比下原生架构持续优于基于编码器的对应模型；全量微调可缩小但不消除差距，单纯扩大规模亦无法弥补；逐层探针显示动作关键信号常在最终视觉表征之前达到峰值；在多种操控场景与分布偏移下，NEO-VLA取得更强且更鲁棒的控制性能。

Conclusion: 具身控制受益于对整个计算层级中多层视觉表征的访问，而非固定编码器输出；"无编码器+多层表征接入"是VLA架构的有效设计方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models are typically built on a simple premise: perception is first compressed by visual encoders, and action reasoning begins only afterwards. Yet manipulation often hinges on precisely the visual signals most vulnerable to this abstraction, e.g., local geometry, boundaries, depth, and subtle state changes. This raises a fundamental question: should action policies inherit a fixed visual endpoint, or directly access evolving visual computation? We introduce NEO-VLA, a structurally encoder-free vision-language-action framework. Images are tokenized by lightweight patch convolutional layers and evolve jointly with language throughout the backbone, allowing a flow-matching action expert to draw from instruction-conditioned visual representations at different depths. This simple change reveals a consistent pattern. Native architectures outperform encoder-based counterparts under controlled comparisons; full fine-tuning reduces but does not erase the gap, and additional scale alone cannot recover it. More strikingly, layer-wise probing shows that the action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. Across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts, NEO-VLA consequently achieves stronger and more robust control. Together, these results suggest that embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy, rather than a fixed encoder output.

</details>
